1
.

This is direct from Theorem
2
.
\square

Proof of Corollary
4
.

This is direct from Theorem
2
.
\square

Proof of Corollary
2
.

This is direct from Theorem
2
.
\square

Proof of Corollary
3
.

This is direct from Theorem
2
.
\square

S4.3
Proofs of theorems

Proof of Theorem
1
.

From (
2.12
) and (
2.13
), we have

\displaystyle\bm{\beta}(\bm{\phi}^{\ast})

\displaystyle=\Big(\mathbf{X}^{\top}\mathbf{B}^{\nu}(\mathbf{B}^{\nu})^{\top}\mathbf{X}\Big)^{-1}\mathbf{X}^{\top}\mathbf{B}^{\nu}(\mathbf{B}^{\nu})^{\top}\Big(\mathbf{y}^{\nu}-\sum_{j=1}^{p}\sum_{k=0}^{l_{j}}\phi_{j,k}^{\ast}\mathbf{W}_{j}^{\otimes}\mathbf{y}_{j,k}^{\nu}\Big)

\displaystyle=\Big(\mathbf{X}^{\top}\mathbf{B}^{\nu}(\mathbf{B}^{\nu})^{\top}\mathbf{X}\Big)^{-1}\mathbf{X}^{\top}\mathbf{B}^{\nu}(\mathbf{B}^{\nu})^{\top}\Big(\mathbf{1}_{T}\otimes\bm{\mu}^{\ast}+\mathbf{X}\bm{\beta}^{\ast}+\bm{\epsilon}^{\nu}\Big)

\displaystyle=\bm{\beta}^{\ast}+\Big(\mathbf{X}^{\top}\mathbf{B}^{\nu}(\mathbf{B}^{\nu})^{\top}\mathbf{X}\Big)^{-1}\mathbf{X}^{\top}\mathbf{B}^{\nu}(\mathbf{B}^{\nu})^{\top}\bm{\epsilon}^{\nu}.

We now define a diagonal matrix
\mathbf{D}_{z_{j,k}}:=\text{diag}(z_{j,k,1}\mathbf{I}_{d},\dots,z_{j,k,T}\mathbf{I}_{d})\in\mathbbm{R}^{dT\times dT}
, with diagonal blocks
z_{j,k,1}\mathbf{I}_{d},\dots,z_{j,k,T}\mathbf{I}_{d}
, and
\bm{\Pi}^{\ast\otimes}:=\big(\mathbf{I}_{Td}-\sum_{j=1}^{p}\sum_{k=0}^{l_{j}}\phi_{j,k}^{\ast}\mathbf{W}_{j}^{\otimes}\mathbf{D}_{z_{j,k}}\big)^{-1}
. We then have
\mathbf{y}^{\nu}=\bm{\Pi}^{\ast\otimes}\big(\mathbf{1}_{T}\otimes\bm{\mu}^{\ast}+\mathbf{X}\bm{\beta}^{\ast}+\bm{\epsilon}^{\nu}\big)
. Thus,

\displaystyle\hskip 14.22636pt\bm{\beta}(\widetilde{\bm{\phi}})=\Big(\mathbf{X}^{\top}\mathbf{B}^{\nu}(\mathbf{B}^{\nu})^{\top}\mathbf{X}\Big)^{-1}\mathbf{X}^{\top}\mathbf{B}^{\nu}(\mathbf{B}^{\nu})^{\top}\Big(\mathbf{y}^{\nu}-\sum_{j=1}^{p}\sum_{k=0}^{l_{j}}\widetilde{\phi}_{j,k}\mathbf{W}_{j}^{\otimes}\mathbf{y}_{j,k}^{\nu}\Big)

\displaystyle=\bm{\beta}(\bm{\phi}^{\ast})+\Big(\mathbf{X}^{\top}\mathbf{B}^{\nu}(\mathbf{B}^{\nu})^{\top}\mathbf{X}\Big)^{-1}\mathbf{X}^{\top}\mathbf{B}^{\nu}(\mathbf{B}^{\nu})^{\top}\Big(\sum_{j=1}^{p}\sum_{k=0}^{l_{j}}(\phi_{j,k}^{\ast}-\widetilde{\phi}_{j,k})\mathbf{W}_{j}^{\otimes}\mathbf{y}_{j,k}^{\nu}\Big)

\displaystyle=\bm{\beta}(\bm{\phi}^{\ast})+\Big(\mathbf{X}^{\top}\mathbf{B}^{\nu}(\mathbf{B}^{\nu})^{\top}\mathbf{X}\Big)^{-1}\mathbf{X}^{\top}\mathbf{B}^{\nu}(\mathbf{B}^{\nu})^{\top}

\displaystyle\hskip 60.0pt\cdot\Big(\sum_{j=1}^{p}\sum_{k=0}^{l_{j}}(\phi_{j,k}^{\ast}-\widetilde{\phi}_{j,k})\mathbf{W}_{j}^{\otimes}\mathbf{D}_{z_{j,k}}\bm{\Pi}^{\ast\otimes}\big(\mathbf{1}_{T}\otimes\bm{\mu}^{\ast}+\mathbf{X}\bm{\beta}^{\ast}+\bm{\epsilon}^{\nu}\big)\Big).

We can hence decompose
\bm{\beta}(\widetilde{\bm{\phi}})-\bm{\beta}^{\ast}=\sum_{j=1}^{5}I_{j}
where

\displaystyle I_{1}:=\big[\mathbbm{E}(\mathbf{X}_{t}^{\top}\mathbf{B}_{t})\mathbbm{E}(\mathbf{B}_{t}^{\top}\mathbf{X}_{t})\big]^{-1}\big[\mathbbm{E}(\mathbf{X}_{t}^{\top}\mathbf{B}_{t})\mathbbm{E}(\mathbf{B}_{t}^{\top}\mathbf{X}_{t})-T^{-2}\mathbf{X}^{\top}\mathbf{B}^{\nu}(\mathbf{B}^{\nu})^{\top}\mathbf{X}\big](\bm{\beta}(\widetilde{\bm{\phi}})-\bm{\beta}^{\ast}),

\displaystyle I_{2}:=\big[\mathbbm{E}(\mathbf{X}_{t}^{\top}\mathbf{B}_{t})\mathbbm{E}(\mathbf{B}_{t}^{\top}\mathbf{X}_{t})\big]^{-1}T^{-2}\mathbf{X}^{\top}\mathbf{B}^{\nu}(\mathbf{B}^{\nu})^{\top}\bm{\epsilon}^{\nu},

\displaystyle I_{3}:=\big[\mathbbm{E}(\mathbf{X}_{t}^{\top}\mathbf{B}_{t})\mathbbm{E}(\mathbf{B}_{t}^{\top}\mathbf{X}_{t})\big]^{-1}T^{-2}\mathbf{X}^{\top}\mathbf{B}^{\nu}(\mathbf{B}^{\nu})^{\top}\sum_{j=1}^{p}\sum_{k=0}^{l_{j}}(\phi_{j,k}^{\ast}-\widetilde{\phi}_{j,k})\mathbf{W}_{j}^{\otimes}\mathbf{D}_{z_{j,k}}\bm{\Pi}^{\ast\otimes}\mathbf{X}\bm{\beta}^{\ast},

\displaystyle I_{4}:=\big[\mathbbm{E}(\mathbf{X}_{t}^{\top}\mathbf{B}_{t})\mathbbm{E}(\mathbf{B}_{t}^{\top}\mathbf{X}_{t})\big]^{-1}T^{-2}\mathbf{X}^{\top}\mathbf{B}^{\nu}(\mathbf{B}^{\nu})^{\top}\sum_{j=1}^{p}\sum_{k=0}^{l_{j}}(\phi_{j,k}^{\ast}-\widetilde{\phi}_{j,k})\mathbf{W}_{j}^{\otimes}\mathbf{D}_{z_{j,k}}\bm{\Pi}^{\ast\otimes}\bm{\epsilon}^{\nu},

\displaystyle I_{5}:=\big[\mathbbm{E}(\mathbf{X}_{t}^{\top}\mathbf{B}_{t})\mathbbm{E}(\mathbf{B}_{t}^{\top}\mathbf{X}_{t})\big]^{-1}T^{-2}\mathbf{X}^{\top}\mathbf{B}^{\nu}(\mathbf{B}^{\nu})^{\top}\sum_{j=1}^{p}\sum_{k=0}^{l_{j}}(\phi_{j,k}^{\ast}-\widetilde{\phi}_{j,k})\mathbf{W}_{j}^{\otimes}\mathbf{D}_{z_{j,k}}\bm{\Pi}^{\ast\otimes}(\mathbf{1}_{T}\otimes\bm{\mu}^{\ast}).

Notice we can take any
t\in[T]
for
\mathbbm{E}(\mathbf{X}_{t}^{\top}\mathbf{B}_{t})
and
\mathbbm{E}(\mathbf{B}_{t}^{\top}\mathbf{X}_{t})
due to Assumption (M1). To bound
I_{1}
to
I_{4}
, we first have

\big\|\big[\mathbbm{E}(\mathbf{X}_{t}^{\top}\mathbf{B}_{t})\mathbbm{E}(\mathbf{B}_{t}^{\top}\mathbf{X}_{t})\big]^{-1}\big\|_{1}\leq\frac{r^{1/2}}{\lambda_{r}\big[\mathbbm{E}(\mathbf{X}_{t}^{\top}\mathbf{B}_{t})\mathbbm{E}(\mathbf{B}_{t}^{\top}\mathbf{X}_{t})\big]}\leq\frac{r^{1/2}}{d^{2}u^{2}},

(S4)

where
\lambda_{r}\big[\mathbbm{E}(\mathbf{X}_{t}^{\top}\mathbf{B}_{t})\mathbbm{E}(\mathbf{B}_{t}^{\top}\mathbf{X}_{t})\big]=\sigma_{r}^{2}(\mathbbm{E}(\mathbf{X}_{t}^{\top}\mathbf{B}_{t}))\geq d^{2}u^{2}
with
u>0
being a constant by Assumption (R3), and
\lambda_{j}(\cdot)
and
\sigma_{j}(\cdot)
denoting the
j
-th largest eigenvalue and singular value of a matrix respectively.

Next, define

\mathbf{U}=\mathbf{I}_{d}\otimes T^{-1}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\text{vec}\big(\mathbf{B}_{t}-\bar{\mathbf{B}}\big)\mathbf{x}_{t}^{\top},\;\;\;\;\;\mathbf{U}_{0}=\mathbf{I}_{d}\otimes\mathbbm{E}(\mathbf{b}_{t}\mathbf{x}_{t}^{\top}).

By Lemma
3
, we then have

\displaystyle T^{-1}\mathbf{X}^{\top}\mathbf{B}^{v}

\displaystyle=T^{-1}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{X}_{t}^{\top}(\mathbf{B}_{t}-\bar{\mathbf{B}})

\displaystyle=T^{-1}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\Big\{\big(\mathbf{I}_{d}\otimes\mathbf{x}_{t}^{\top}\big)\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{I}_{d},r}\Big\}^{\top}\Big\{\big(\mathbf{I}_{d}\otimes\text{vec}\big(\mathbf{B}_{t}-\bar{\mathbf{B}}\big)^{\top}\big)\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{I}_{d},v}\Big\}

\displaystyle=T^{-1}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{I}_{d},r}^{\top}\big(\mathbf{I}_{d}\otimes\mathbf{x}_{t}\big)\big(\mathbf{I}_{d}\otimes\text{vec}\big(\mathbf{B}_{t}-\bar{\mathbf{B}}\big)^{\top}\big)\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{I}_{d},v}=\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{I}_{d},r}^{\top}\mathbf{U}^{\top}\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{I}_{d},v}.

Similarly we have
\mathbbm{E}(\mathbf{X}_{t}^{\top}\mathbf{B}_{t})=\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{I}_{d},r}^{\top}\mathbf{U}_{0}^{\top}\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{I}_{d},v}
. Thus on the set
\mathcal{M}
in Lemma
2
, with (
S4
) it holds that

\begin{split}&\hskip 12.0pt\big\|I_{1}\big\|_{1}\leq\frac{r^{1/2}}{d^{2}u^{2}}\cdot\big\|\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{I}_{d},r}^{\top}\mathbf{U}_{0}^{\top}\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{I}_{d},v}\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{I}_{d},v}^{\top}\mathbf{U}_{0}-\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{I}_{d},r}^{\top}\mathbf{U}^{\top}\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{I}_{d},v}\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{I}_{d},v}^{\top}\mathbf{U}\big\|_{1}\cdot\big\|\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{I}_{d},r}(\bm{\beta}(\widetilde{\bm{\phi}})-\bm{\beta}^{\ast})\big\|_{1}\\
&=O\Big(\frac{1}{d}\Big)\Big(\big\|\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{I}_{d},r}^{\top}(\mathbf{U}_{0}-\mathbf{U})^{\top}\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{I}_{d},v}\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{I}_{d},v}^{\top}\mathbf{U}_{0}\big\|_{1}+\big\|\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{I}_{d},r}^{\top}\mathbf{U}^{\top}\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{I}_{d},v}\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{I}_{d},v}^{\top}(\mathbf{U}_{0}-\mathbf{U})\big\|_{1}\Big)\,\big\|\bm{\beta}(\widetilde{\bm{\phi}})-\bm{\beta}^{\ast}\big\|_{1}\\
&=O\Big(\frac{1}{d}\Big)\Big\{d\cdot\big\|\mathbf{U}_{0}-\mathbf{U}\big\|_{\text{max}}\cdot\big\|\mathbf{U}_{0}\big\|_{\text{max}}\\
&\hskip 12.0pt+d\cdot\big\|\mathbf{U}_{0}-\mathbf{U}\big\|_{\text{max}}\cdot\Big(\big\|\mathbf{U}_{0}-\mathbf{U}\big\|_{\text{max}}+\big\|\mathbf{U}_{0}\big\|_{\text{max}}\Big)\Big\}\cdot\big\|\bm{\beta}(\widetilde{\bm{\phi}})-\bm{\beta}^{\ast}\big\|_{1}=O\Big(c_{T}\big\|\bm{\beta}(\widetilde{\bm{\phi}})-\bm{\beta}^{\ast}\big\|_{1}\Big),\end{split}

(S5)

where the last equality used Assumption (R3) which implies
\big\|\mathbf{U}_{0}\big\|_{\text{max}}
is bounded by some constant, and Lemma
2
(using
\mathcal{A}_{1},\,\mathcal{A}_{4},\,\mathcal{A}_{6}
) that

\begin{split}&\hskip 12.0pt\big\|\mathbf{U}-\mathbf{U}_{0}\big\|_{\text{max}}=\bigg\|\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\big[\text{vec}\big(\mathbf{B}_{t}-\bar{\mathbf{B}}\big)\mathbf{x}_{t}^{\top}\big]-\mathbbm{E}(\mathbf{b}_{t}\mathbf{x}_{t}^{\top})\bigg\|_{\text{max}}\\
&=\bigg\|\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{b}_{t}\mathbf{x}_{t}^{\top}-\mathbbm{E}(\mathbf{b}_{t}\mathbf{x}_{t}^{\top})-\text{vec}\big(\bar{\mathbf{B}}\big)\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{x}_{t}^{\top}\bigg\|_{\text{max}}\leq c_{T}+\bigg\|\text{vec}\big(\bar{\mathbf{B}}\big)\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{x}_{t}^{\top}\bigg\|_{\text{max}}\\
&\leq c_{T}+\Big(\big\|\text{vec}\big(\bar{\mathbf{B}}\big)-\mathbbm{E}[\mathbf{B}_{t}]\big\|_{\text{max}}+\big\|\mathbbm{E}[\mathbf{B}_{t}]\big\|_{\text{max}}\Big)\bigg\|\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{x}_{t}^{\top}\bigg\|_{\text{max}}\leq c_{T}+c_{T}(c_{T}+\mu_{b,\text{max}}).\end{split}

(S6)

Similarly for
I_{2}
, we have on the set
\mathcal{M}
that

\begin{split}&\hskip 12.0pt\big\|I_{2}\big\|_{1}\leq\frac{r^{1/2}}{d^{2}u^{2}}\cdot\big\|\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{I}_{d},r}^{\top}\mathbf{U}^{\top}\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{I}_{d},v}\big\|_{1}\cdot\big\|T^{-1}(\mathbf{B}^{\nu})^{\top}\bm{\epsilon}^{\nu}\big\|_{1}\\
&=O\Big(\frac{1}{d}\Big)\big\|T^{-1}(\mathbf{B}^{\nu})^{\top}\bm{\epsilon}^{\nu}\big\|_{1}=O\Big(\frac{1}{d}\Big)\bigg\|\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}(\mathbf{B}_{t}^{\top}-\bar{\mathbf{B}}^{\top})\bm{\epsilon}_{t}\bigg\|_{1}\\
&=O_{p}\Big(\frac{1}{d}\Big)\Big(c_{T}d^{\frac{1}{2}+\frac{1}{2w}}+c_{T}d^{1/2}\log^{1/2}(T\vee d)S_{\epsilon}(\mu_{b,\text{max}}+c_{T})\Big)=O_{p}\big(c_{T}d^{-\frac{1}{2}+\frac{1}{2w}}\big),\end{split}

(S7)

where the first equality used
\mathcal{A}_{1},\,\mathcal{A}_{4},\,\mathcal{A}_{6}
in
\mathcal{M}
, the third used
\mathcal{A}_{3},\,\mathcal{A}_{7}
in
\mathcal{M}
, and the last used Assumption (R10).

For
I_{3}
, first recall that

\bm{\Pi}_{t}^{\ast}=(\mathbf{I}_{d}-\mathbf{W}_{t}^{\ast})^{-1}=\big(\mathbf{I}_{d}-\sum_{j=1}^{p}\sum_{k=0}^{l_{j}}\phi_{j,k}^{\ast}z_{j,k,t}\mathbf{W}_{j}\big)^{-1},

(S8)

and hence from Assumption (M2) (resp. (M2’)) we have
\|\bm{\Pi}_{t}^{\ast}\|_{\infty}\leq 1/(1-\eta)=O(1)
(resp.
\|\bm{\Pi}_{t}^{\ast}\|_{\infty}=O_{P}(1)
) using that
(\mathbf{I}_{d}-\mathbf{W}_{t}^{\ast})
is strictly diagonally dominant. Then by (
S4
), we have on the set
\mathcal{M}
that

\begin{split}&\hskip 12.0pt\big\|I_{3}\big\|_{1}\leq\frac{r^{1/2}}{d^{2}u^{2}}\cdot\big\|\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{I}_{d},r}^{\top}\mathbf{U}^{\top}\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{I}_{d},v}\big\|_{1}\cdot\bigg\|T^{-1}(\mathbf{B}^{\nu})^{\top}\sum_{j=1}^{p}\sum_{k=0}^{l_{j}}(\phi_{j,k}^{\ast}-\widetilde{\phi}_{j,k})\mathbf{W}_{j}^{\otimes}\mathbf{D}_{z_{j,k}}\bm{\Pi}^{\ast\otimes}\mathbf{X}\bigg\|_{1}\cdot\big\|\bm{\beta}^{\ast}\big\|_{1}\\
&=O\Big(\frac{1}{d}\Big)\bigg\|\frac{1}{T}\sum_{j=1}^{p}\sum_{k=0}^{l_{j}}(\phi_{j,k}^{\ast}-\widetilde{\phi}_{j,k})(\mathbf{B}^{\nu})^{\top}\mathbf{W}_{j}^{\otimes}\mathbf{D}_{z_{j,k}}\bm{\Pi}^{\ast\otimes}\mathbf{X}\bigg\|_{1}\\
&=O\Big(\frac{1}{d}\Big)\bigg\|\frac{1}{T}\sum_{j=1}^{p}\sum_{k=0}^{l_{j}}(\phi_{j,k}^{\ast}-\widetilde{\phi}_{j,k})\sum_{t=1}^{T}z_{j,k,t}(\mathbf{B}_{t}^{\top}-\bar{\mathbf{B}}^{\top})\mathbf{W}_{j}\bm{\Pi}_{t}^{\ast}\mathbf{X}_{t}\bigg\|_{1}\\
&=O\Big(\frac{1}{d}\Big)\bigg\{\max_{q\in[r]}\max_{s\in[v]}\Big|\frac{1}{T}\sum_{j=1}^{p}\sum_{k=0}^{l_{j}}(\phi_{j,k}^{\ast}-\widetilde{\phi}_{j,k})\sum_{t=1}^{T}\sum_{i=1}^{d}z_{j,k,t}\mathbf{W}_{j,\cdot i}^{\top}(\mathbf{B}_{t,\cdot s}-\bar{\mathbf{B}}_{\cdot s})\mathbf{X}_{t,\cdot q}^{\top}\bm{\Pi}_{t,i\cdot}^{\ast}\Big|\bigg\}\\
&=O\Big(\frac{1}{d}\Big)\Big(\big\|\bm{\phi}^{\ast}-\widetilde{\bm{\phi}}\big\|_{1}\\
&\hskip 40.0pt\cdot\max_{q\in[r],s\in[v],j\in[p]}\max_{k\in[l_{j}]\cup\{0\}}\max_{m,n\in[d]}\Big|\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}z_{j,k,t}(B_{t,ms}-\bar{B}_{ms})X_{t,nq}\Big|\sum_{i=1}^{d}\|\mathbf{W}_{j,\cdot i}\|_{1}\|\bm{\Pi}_{t,i\cdot}^{\ast}\|_{1}\Big)\\
&=O_{p}\Big(\big\|\bm{\phi}^{\ast}-\widetilde{\bm{\phi}}\big\|_{1}\big[c_{T}+1+c_{T}(c_{T}+\mu_{b,\text{max}})\big]\Big)=O_{p}\Big(\big\|\bm{\phi}^{\ast}-\widetilde{\bm{\phi}}\big\|_{1}\Big),\end{split}

(S9)

where the first equality used
\mathcal{A}_{1},\,\mathcal{A}_{4},\,\mathcal{A}_{6}
in
\mathcal{M}
, the second last used
\mathcal{A}_{4},\,\mathcal{A}_{8},\,\mathcal{A}_{9}
in
\mathcal{M}
and Assumptions (R1) and (R3). Similarly, for
I_{4}
on the set
\mathcal{M}
,

\begin{split}&\hskip 12.0pt\big\|I_{4}\big\|_{1}\leq\frac{r^{1/2}}{d^{2}u^{2}}\cdot\big\|\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{I}_{d},r}^{\top}\mathbf{U}^{\top}\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{I}_{d},v}\big\|_{1}\cdot\big\|T^{-1}(\mathbf{B}^{\nu})^{\top}\sum_{j=1}^{p}\sum_{k=0}^{l_{j}}(\phi_{j,k}^{\ast}-\widetilde{\phi}_{j,k})\mathbf{W}_{j}^{\otimes}\mathbf{D}_{z_{j,k}}\bm{\Pi}^{\ast\otimes}\bm{\epsilon}^{\nu}\big\|_{1}\\
&=O\Big(\frac{1}{d}\Big)\bigg\{\max_{s\in[v]}\Big|\frac{1}{T}\sum_{j=1}^{p}\sum_{k=0}^{l_{j}}(\phi_{j,k}^{\ast}-\widetilde{\phi}_{j,k})\sum_{t=1}^{T}\sum_{i=1}^{d}z_{j,k,t}\mathbf{W}_{j,\cdot i}^{\top}(\mathbf{B}_{t,\cdot s}-\bar{\mathbf{B}}_{\cdot s})\bm{\epsilon}_{t}^{\top}\bm{\Pi}_{t,i\cdot}^{\ast}\Big|\bigg\}\\
&=O\Big(\frac{1}{d}\Big)\bigg\{\big\|\bm{\phi}^{\ast}-\widetilde{\bm{\phi}}\big\|_{1}\\
&\hskip 50.0pt\cdot\max_{s\in[v]}\max_{j\in[p]}\max_{k\in[l_{j}]\cup\{0\}}\max_{m,q\in[d]}\Big|\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}z_{j,k,t}(B_{t,ms}-\bar{B}_{ms})\epsilon_{t,q}\Big|\,\sum_{i=1}^{d}\|\mathbf{W}_{j,\cdot i}\|_{1}\|\bm{\Pi}_{t,i\cdot}^{\ast}\|_{1}\bigg\}\\
&=O_{p}\Big(\big\|\bm{\phi}^{\ast}-\widetilde{\bm{\phi}}\big\|_{1}\big[c_{T}+c_{T}(c_{T}+\mu_{b,\text{max}})\big]\Big)=O_{p}\Big(c_{T}\big\|\bm{\phi}^{\ast}-\widetilde{\bm{\phi}}\big\|_{1}\Big),\end{split}

(S10)

where the first equality used
\mathcal{A}_{1},\,\mathcal{A}_{4},\,\mathcal{A}_{6}
in
\mathcal{M}
, the second last used
\mathcal{A}_{4},\,\mathcal{A}_{10},\,\mathcal{A}_{11}
in
\mathcal{M}
and Assumptions (R1) and (R3). For
I_{5}
we also have on the set
\mathcal{M}
,

\begin{split}&\hskip 12.0pt\big\|I_{5}\big\|_{1}\leq\frac{r^{1/2}}{d^{2}u^{2}}\cdot\big\|\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{I}_{d},r}^{\top}\mathbf{U}^{\top}\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{I}_{d},v}\big\|_{1}\cdot\big\|T^{-1}(\mathbf{B}^{\nu})^{\top}\sum_{j=1}^{p}\sum_{k=0}^{l_{j}}(\phi_{j,k}^{\ast}-\widetilde{\phi}_{j,k})\mathbf{W}_{j}^{\otimes}\mathbf{D}_{z_{j,k}}\bm{\Pi}^{\ast\otimes}(\mathbf{1}_{T}\otimes\bm{\mu}^{\ast})\big\|_{1}\\
&=O\Big(\frac{1}{d}\Big)\bigg\{\max_{s\in[v]}\Big|\frac{1}{T}\sum_{j=1}^{p}\sum_{k=0}^{l_{j}}(\phi_{j,k}^{\ast}-\widetilde{\phi}_{j,k})\sum_{t=1}^{T}\sum_{i=1}^{d}z_{j,k,t}\mathbf{W}_{j,\cdot i}^{\top}(\mathbf{B}_{t,\cdot s}-\bar{\mathbf{B}}_{\cdot s})\bm{\mu}^{\ast\top}\bm{\Pi}_{t,i\cdot}^{\ast}\Big|\bigg\}\\
&=O\Big(\frac{1}{d}\Big)\bigg\{\big\|\bm{\phi}^{\ast}-\widetilde{\bm{\phi}}\big\|_{1}\\
&\hskip 40.0pt\cdot\max_{s\in[v]}\max_{j\in[p]}\max_{k\in[l_{j}]\cup\{0\}}\max_{m\in[d]}\Big|\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}z_{j,k,t}(B_{t,ms}-\bar{B}_{ms})\Big|\,\|\bm{\mu}^{\ast}\|_{\text{max}}\,\sum_{i=1}^{d}\|\mathbf{W}_{j,\cdot i}\|_{1}\|\bm{\Pi}_{t,i\cdot}^{\ast}\|_{1}\bigg\}\\
&=O_{p}\Big(\big\|\bm{\phi}^{\ast}-\widetilde{\bm{\phi}}\big\|_{1}\big[c_{T}+(z_{\text{max}}c_{T}\vee c_{T})\big]\Big)=O_{p}\Big(c_{T}\big\|\bm{\phi}^{\ast}-\widetilde{\bm{\phi}}\big\|_{1}\Big),\end{split}

(S11)

where the first equality used
\mathcal{A}_{1},\,\mathcal{A}_{4},\,\mathcal{A}_{6}
in
\mathcal{M}
, the second last used
\mathcal{A}_{4},\,\mathcal{A}_{12},\,\mathcal{A}_{13}
in
\mathcal{M}
and Assumptions (R1),
(R3), and
\mathbbm{E}(z_{j,k,t}\mathbf{B}_{t})=\mathbf{0}
from (M2’) if (M2) is not satisfied.

From (
S5
), (
S7
), (
S9
) and (
S10
), combining with Lemma
2
, we have

\big\|\bm{\beta}(\widetilde{\bm{\phi}})-\bm{\beta}^{\ast}\big\|_{1}\leq\sum_{j=1}^{5}\big\|I_{j}\big\|_{1}=O_{p}\Big(\big\|\widetilde{\bm{\phi}}-\bm{\phi}^{\ast}\big\|_{1}+c_{T}d^{-\frac{1}{2}+\frac{1}{2w}}\Big).

(S12)

For the remaining of the proof of
1
, we work on the rate for
\big\|\widetilde{\bm{\phi}}-\bm{\phi}^{\ast}\big\|_{1}
. From (
2.14
), we have

\displaystyle\widetilde{\bm{\phi}}

\displaystyle=\big[(\mathbf{B}^{\top}\mathbf{V}-\bm{\Xi}\mathbf{Y}_{W})^{\top}(\mathbf{B}^{\top}\mathbf{V}-\bm{\Xi}\mathbf{Y}_{W})\big]^{-1}(\mathbf{B}^{\top}\mathbf{V}-\bm{\Xi}\mathbf{Y}_{W})^{\top}(\mathbf{B}^{\top}\mathbf{y}-\bm{\Xi}\mathbf{y}^{\nu})

\displaystyle=\big[(\mathbf{B}^{\top}\mathbf{V}-\bm{\Xi}\mathbf{Y}_{W})^{\top}(\mathbf{B}^{\top}\mathbf{V}-\bm{\Xi}\mathbf{Y}_{W})\big]^{-1}(\mathbf{B}^{\top}\mathbf{V}-\bm{\Xi}\mathbf{Y}_{W})^{\top}(\mathbf{B}^{\top}\mathbf{X}_{\bm{\beta}^{\ast}}\vec{\mathbf{I}_{d}}+\mathbf{B}^{\top}\bm{\epsilon}-\bm{\Xi}\mathbf{y}^{\nu})

\displaystyle\hskip 12.0pt+\big[(\mathbf{B}^{\top}\mathbf{V}-\bm{\Xi}\mathbf{Y}_{W})^{\top}(\mathbf{B}^{\top}\mathbf{V}-\bm{\Xi}\mathbf{Y}_{W})\big]^{-1}(\mathbf{B}^{\top}\mathbf{V}-\bm{\Xi}\mathbf{Y}_{W})^{\top}(\bm{\Xi}\mathbf{Y}_{W})\bm{\phi}^{\ast}+\bm{\phi}^{\ast}

\displaystyle=\bm{\phi}^{\ast}+\big[(\mathbf{B}^{\top}\mathbf{V}-\bm{\Xi}\mathbf{Y}_{W})^{\top}(\mathbf{B}^{\top}\mathbf{V}-\bm{\Xi}\mathbf{Y}_{W})\big]^{-1}(\mathbf{B}^{\top}\mathbf{V}-\bm{\Xi}\mathbf{Y}_{W})^{\top}\mathbf{B}^{\top}\bm{\epsilon}

\displaystyle\hskip 12.0pt-T^{-1/2}d^{-a/2}\cdot\big[(\mathbf{B}^{\top}\mathbf{V}-\bm{\Xi}\mathbf{Y}_{W})^{\top}(\mathbf{B}^{\top}\mathbf{V}-\bm{\Xi}\mathbf{Y}_{W})\big]^{-1}(\mathbf{B}^{\top}\mathbf{V}-\bm{\Xi}\mathbf{Y}_{W})^{\top}

\displaystyle\hskip 12.0pt\cdot\text{vec}\bigg\{\sum_{t=1}^{T}(\mathbf{B}_{t}-\bar{\mathbf{B}})\bm{\gamma}(\bm{\epsilon}^{\nu})^{\top}\mathbf{B}^{\nu}(\mathbf{B}^{\nu})^{\top}\mathbf{X}(\mathbf{X}^{\top}\mathbf{B}^{\nu}(\mathbf{B}^{\nu})^{\top}\mathbf{X})^{-1}\mathbf{X}_{t}^{\top}\bigg\},

where the second equality used (
2.11
), and the third used the fact that
\mathbf{B}^{\top}\mathbf{X}_{\bm{\beta}(\bm{\phi})}\vec{\mathbf{I}_{d}}=\bm{\Xi}\mathbf{y}^{\nu}-\bm{\Xi}\mathbf{Y}_{W}\bm{\phi}
from (
S3
) and
\bm{\beta}(\bm{\phi}^{\ast})=\bm{\beta}^{\ast}+\big(\mathbf{X}^{\top}\mathbf{B}^{\nu}(\mathbf{B}^{\nu})^{\top}\mathbf{X}\big)^{-1}\mathbf{X}^{\top}\mathbf{B}^{\nu}(\mathbf{B}^{\nu})^{\top}\bm{\epsilon}^{\nu}
. Thus, we may decompose

\displaystyle\widetilde{\bm{\phi}}-\bm{\phi}^{\ast}

\displaystyle=D_{1}-D_{2},\,\,\,\text{where}

\displaystyle D_{1}

\displaystyle=\big[(\mathbf{B}^{\top}\mathbf{V}-\bm{\Xi}\mathbf{Y}_{W})^{\top}(\mathbf{B}^{\top}\mathbf{V}-\bm{\Xi}\mathbf{Y}_{W})\big]^{-1}(\mathbf{B}^{\top}\mathbf{V}-\bm{\Xi}\mathbf{Y}_{W})^{\top}\mathbf{B}^{\top}\bm{\epsilon},

\displaystyle D_{2}

\displaystyle=T^{-1/2}d^{-a/2}\cdot\big[(\mathbf{B}^{\top}\mathbf{V}-\bm{\Xi}\mathbf{Y}_{W})^{\top}(\mathbf{B}^{\top}\mathbf{V}-\bm{\Xi}\mathbf{Y}_{W})\big]^{-1}(\mathbf{B}^{\top}\mathbf{V}-\bm{\Xi}\mathbf{Y}_{W})^{\top}

\displaystyle\hskip 12.0pt\cdot\bigg\{\sum_{t=1}^{T}\Big(\mathbf{I}_{d}\otimes(\mathbf{B}_{t}-\bar{\mathbf{B}})\bm{\gamma}\Big)\mathbf{X}_{t}\bigg\}(\mathbf{X}^{\top}\mathbf{B}^{\nu}(\mathbf{B}^{\nu})^{\top}\mathbf{X})^{-1}\mathbf{X}^{\top}\mathbf{B}^{\nu}(\mathbf{B}^{\nu})^{\top}\bm{\epsilon}^{\nu}.

To bound the above, recall first the following definitions (from the statement of Theorem
2
) for
j\in[p],k\in[l_{j}]\cup\{0\}
,

\displaystyle\mathbf{U}_{\mathbf{x},j,k}:=\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}z_{j,k,t}\text{vec}\big(\mathbf{B}_{t}-\bar{\mathbf{B}}\big)\mathbf{x}_{t}^{\top},\,\,\,\mathbf{U}_{\bm{\mu},j,k}:=\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}z_{j,k,t}\text{vec}\big(\mathbf{B}_{t}-\bar{\mathbf{B}}\big)\bm{\mu}^{\ast\top},

\displaystyle\mathbf{U}_{\bm{\epsilon},j,k}:=\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}z_{j,k,t}\text{vec}\big(\mathbf{B}_{t}-\bar{\mathbf{B}}\big)\bm{\epsilon}_{t}^{\top}.

Consider
\bm{\Xi}\mathbf{Y}_{W}
, from its definition we have,

\displaystyle\bm{\Xi}\mathbf{Y}_{W}

\displaystyle=T^{-1/2}d^{-a/2}\Big(\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{X}_{t}\otimes(\mathbf{B}_{t}-\bar{\mathbf{B}})\bm{\gamma}\Big)\big[\mathbf{X}^{\top}\mathbf{B}^{\nu}(\mathbf{B}^{\nu})^{\top}\mathbf{X}\big]^{-1}\mathbf{X}^{\top}\mathbf{B}^{\nu}(\mathbf{B}^{\nu})^{\top}

\displaystyle\hskip 12.0pt\cdot(\mathbf{W}_{1}^{\otimes}\mathbf{y}_{1,0}^{\nu},\dots,\mathbf{W}_{1}^{\otimes}\mathbf{y}_{1,l_{1}}^{\nu},\dots,\mathbf{W}_{p}^{\otimes}\mathbf{y}_{p,0}^{\nu},\dots,\mathbf{W}_{p}^{\otimes}\mathbf{y}_{p,l_{p}}^{\nu})

\displaystyle=T^{-1/2}d^{-a/2}\Big(\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{X}_{t}\otimes(\mathbf{B}_{t}-\bar{\mathbf{B}})\bm{\gamma}\Big)\big[\mathbf{X}^{\top}\mathbf{B}^{\nu}(\mathbf{B}^{\nu})^{\top}\mathbf{X}\big]^{-1}\mathbf{X}^{\top}\mathbf{B}^{\nu}

\displaystyle\hskip 12.0pt\cdot\Bigg\{\sum_{t=1}^{T}z_{1,0,t}(\mathbf{B}_{t}-\bar{\mathbf{B}})^{\top}\mathbf{W}_{1}\mathbf{y}_{t},\cdots,\sum_{t=1}^{T}z_{p,l_{p},t}(\mathbf{B}_{t}-\bar{\mathbf{B}})^{\top}\mathbf{W}_{p}\mathbf{y}_{t}\Bigg\}

From (
S8
), we have
\mathbf{y}_{t}=\bm{\Pi}_{t}^{\ast}\bm{\mu}^{\ast}+\bm{\Pi}_{t}^{\ast}\mathbf{X}_{t}\bm{\beta}^{\ast}+\bm{\Pi}_{t}^{\ast}\bm{\epsilon}_{t}
. It hence holds for any
j\in[p],k\in[l_{j}]\cup\{0\}
by Lemma
3
that

\begin{split}&\hskip 12.0pt\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}z_{j,k,t}(\mathbf{B}_{t}-\bar{\mathbf{B}})^{\top}\mathbf{W}_{j}\mathbf{y}_{t}\\
&=\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}z_{j,k,t}\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{W}_{j}^{\top},v}^{\top}\big(\mathbf{I}_{d}\otimes\text{vec}\big(\mathbf{B}_{t}-\bar{\mathbf{B}}\big)\big)(\bm{\Pi}_{t}^{\ast}\bm{\mu}^{\ast}+\bm{\Pi}_{t}^{\ast}\mathbf{X}_{t}\bm{\beta}^{\ast}+\bm{\Pi}_{t}^{\ast}\bm{\epsilon}_{t})\\
&=\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{W}_{j}^{\top},v}^{\top}(\mathbf{I}_{d}\otimes\mathbf{U}_{\mathbf{x},j,k})\mathbf{V}_{\bm{\Pi}_{t}^{\ast},r}\bm{\beta}^{\ast}+\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{W}_{j}^{\top},v}^{\top}(\mathbf{I}_{d}\otimes\mathbf{U}_{\bm{\mu},j,k})\text{vec}\big(\bm{\Pi}_{t}^{\ast\top}\big)+\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{W}_{j}^{\top},v}^{\top}(\mathbf{I}_{d}\otimes\mathbf{U}_{\bm{\epsilon},j,k})\text{vec}\big(\bm{\Pi}_{t}^{\ast\top}\big).\end{split}

(S13)

Consider also
\mathbf{B}^{\top}\mathbf{V}
, we have

\displaystyle\hskip 12.0pt\mathbf{B}^{\top}\mathbf{V}=T^{-1/2}d^{-a/2}\Big(\mathbf{I}_{d}\otimes\big\{(\mathbf{B}_{1}-\bar{\mathbf{B}},\dots,\mathbf{B}_{T}-\bar{\mathbf{B}})(\mathbf{I}_{T}\otimes\bm{\gamma})\big\}\Big)

\displaystyle\hskip 12.0pt\cdot\Bigg\{\Big[\mathbf{I}_{d}\otimes(z_{1,0,1}\mathbf{y}_{1},\dots,z_{1,0,T}\mathbf{y}_{T})^{\top}\Big]\text{vec}\big(\mathbf{W}_{1}^{\top}\big),\dots,\Big[\mathbf{I}_{d}\otimes(z_{1,l_{1},1}\mathbf{y}_{1},\dots,z_{1,l_{1},T}\mathbf{y}_{T})^{\top}\Big]\vec{\mathbf{W}_{1}^{\top}},

\displaystyle\hskip 12.0pt\dots,\Big[\mathbf{I}_{d}\otimes(z_{p,0,1}\mathbf{y}_{1},\dots,z_{p,0,T}\mathbf{y}_{T})^{\top}\Big]\text{vec}\big(\mathbf{W}_{p}^{\top}\big),\dots,\Big[\mathbf{I}_{d}\otimes(z_{p,l_{p},1}\mathbf{y}_{1},\dots,z_{p,l_{p},T}\mathbf{y}_{T})^{\top}\Big]\text{vec}\big(\mathbf{W}_{p}^{\top}\big)\Bigg\}

\displaystyle=T^{-1/2}d^{-a/2}\Bigg\{\Big[\mathbf{I}_{d}\otimes(\mathbf{B}_{1}-\bar{\mathbf{B}},\dots,\mathbf{B}_{T}-\bar{\mathbf{B}})(\mathbf{I}_{T}\otimes\bm{\gamma})(z_{1,0,1}\mathbf{y}_{1},\dots,z_{1,0,T}\mathbf{y}_{T})^{\top}\Big]\text{vec}\big(\mathbf{W}_{1}^{\top}\big),

\displaystyle\hskip 12.0pt\dots,\Big[\mathbf{I}_{d}\otimes(\mathbf{B}_{1}-\bar{\mathbf{B}},\dots,\mathbf{B}_{T}-\bar{\mathbf{B}})(\mathbf{I}_{T}\otimes\bm{\gamma})(z_{p,l_{p},1}\mathbf{y}_{1},\dots,z_{p,l_{p},T}\mathbf{y}_{T})^{\top}\Big]\text{vec}\big(\mathbf{W}_{p}^{\top}\big)\Bigg\}

\displaystyle=T^{-1/2}d^{-a/2}\Bigg\{\Big[\mathbf{I}_{d}\otimes\sum_{t=1}^{T}z_{1,0,t}(\mathbf{B}_{t}-\bar{\mathbf{B}})\bm{\gamma}\mathbf{y}_{t}^{\top}\Big]\vec{\mathbf{W}_{1}^{\top}},

\displaystyle\hskip 80.0pt\dots,\Big[\mathbf{I}_{d}\otimes\sum_{t=1}^{T}z_{p,l_{p},t}(\mathbf{B}_{t}-\bar{\mathbf{B}})\bm{\gamma}\mathbf{y}_{t}^{\top}\Big]\text{vec}\big(\mathbf{W}_{p}^{\top}\big)\Bigg\}.

Similar to
\bm{\Xi}\mathbf{Y}_{W}
, for
j\in[p],k\in[l_{p}]\cup\{0\}
we have

\begin{split}&\hskip 12.0pt\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}z_{j,k,t}(\mathbf{B}_{t}-\bar{\mathbf{B}})\bm{\gamma}\mathbf{y}_{t}^{\top}=\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}z_{j,k,t}(\mathbf{B}_{t}-\bar{\mathbf{B}})\bm{\gamma}(\bm{\mu}^{\ast\top}\bm{\Pi}_{t}^{\ast\top}+\bm{\beta}^{\ast\top}\mathbf{X}_{t}^{\top}\bm{\Pi}_{t}^{\ast\top}+\bm{\epsilon}_{t}^{\top}\bm{\Pi}_{t}^{\ast\top})\\
&=\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}z_{j,k,t}(\bm{\gamma}^{\top}\otimes\mathbf{I}_{d})\,\text{vec}\big(\mathbf{B}_{t}-\bar{\mathbf{B}}\big)\bm{\mu}^{\ast\top}\bm{\Pi}_{t}^{\ast\top}+\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}z_{j,k,t}(\bm{\gamma}^{\top}\otimes\mathbf{I}_{d})\,\text{vec}\big(\mathbf{B}_{t}-\bar{\mathbf{B}}\big)\mathbf{x}_{t}^{\top}(\bm{\beta}^{\ast}\otimes\mathbf{I}_{d})\bm{\Pi}_{t}^{\ast\top}\\
&\hskip 12.0pt+\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}z_{j,k,t}(\bm{\gamma}^{\top}\otimes\mathbf{I}_{d})\,\text{vec}\big(\mathbf{B}_{t}-\bar{\mathbf{B}}\big)\bm{\epsilon}_{t}^{\top}\bm{\Pi}_{t}^{\ast\top}\\
&=(\bm{\gamma}^{\top}\otimes\mathbf{I}_{d})\mathbf{U}_{\bm{\mu},j,k}\bm{\Pi}_{t}^{\ast\top}+(\bm{\gamma}^{\top}\otimes\mathbf{I}_{d})\mathbf{U}_{\mathbf{x},j,k}(\bm{\beta}^{\ast}\otimes\mathbf{I}_{d})\bm{\Pi}_{t}^{\ast\top}+(\bm{\gamma}^{\top}\otimes\mathbf{I}_{d})\mathbf{U}_{\bm{\epsilon},j,k}\bm{\Pi}_{t}^{\ast\top}.\end{split}

(S14)

With (
S13
) and (
S14
), recall the following in the statement of Theorem
2
,

\displaystyle\mathbf{H}_{10}

\displaystyle=\Big\{\Big(\mathbf{I}_{d}\otimes(\bm{\gamma}^{\top}\otimes\mathbf{I}_{d})\mathbbm{E}\big(\mathbf{U}_{\mathbf{x},1,0}(\bm{\beta}^{\ast}\otimes\mathbf{I}_{d})\bm{\Pi}_{t}^{\ast\top}\big)\Big)\textnormal{vec}(\mathbf{W}_{1}^{\top})

\displaystyle\hskip 24.0pt+\Big(\mathbf{I}_{d}\otimes(\bm{\gamma}^{\top}\otimes\mathbf{I}_{d})\mathbbm{E}\big(\mathbf{U}_{\bm{\mu},1,0}\bm{\Pi}_{t}^{\ast\top}\big)\Big)\textnormal{vec}(\mathbf{W}_{1}^{\top}),

\displaystyle\hskip 12.0pt\dots,\Big(\mathbf{I}_{d}\otimes(\bm{\gamma}^{\top}\otimes\mathbf{I}_{d})\mathbbm{E}\big(\mathbf{U}_{\mathbf{x},p,l_{p}}(\bm{\beta}^{\ast}\otimes\mathbf{I}_{d})\bm{\Pi}_{t}^{\ast\top}\big)\Big)\textnormal{vec}(\mathbf{W}_{p}^{\top})

\displaystyle\hskip 24.0pt+\Big(\mathbf{I}_{d}\otimes(\bm{\gamma}^{\top}\otimes\mathbf{I}_{d})\mathbbm{E}\big(\mathbf{U}_{\bm{\mu},p,l_{p}}\bm{\Pi}_{t}^{\ast\top}\big)\Big)\textnormal{vec}(\mathbf{W}_{p}^{\top})\Big\},

\displaystyle\mathbf{H}_{20}

\displaystyle=\mathbbm{E}(\mathbf{X}_{t}\otimes\mathbf{B}_{t}\bm{\gamma})\big[\mathbbm{E}(\mathbf{X}_{t}^{\top}\mathbf{B}_{t})\mathbbm{E}(\mathbf{B}_{t}^{\top}\mathbf{X}_{t})\big]^{-1}\mathbbm{E}(\mathbf{X}_{t}^{\top}\mathbf{B}_{t})

\displaystyle\hskip 12.0pt\cdot\Big\{\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{W}_{1}^{\top},v}^{\top}\mathbbm{E}\big[(\mathbf{I}_{d}\otimes\mathbf{U}_{\mathbf{x},1,0})\mathbf{V}_{\bm{\Pi}_{t}^{\ast},r}\big]\bm{\beta}^{\ast}+\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{W}_{1}^{\top},v}^{\top}\mathbbm{E}\big[(\mathbf{I}_{d}\otimes\mathbf{U}_{\bm{\mu},1,0})\textnormal{vec}(\bm{\Pi}_{t}^{\ast\top})\big],

\displaystyle\hskip 12.0pt\dots,\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{W}_{p}^{\top},v}^{\top}\mathbbm{E}\big[(\mathbf{I}_{d}\otimes\mathbf{U}_{\mathbf{x},p,l_{p}})\mathbf{V}_{\bm{\Pi}_{t}^{\ast},r}\big]\bm{\beta}^{\ast}+\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{W}_{p}^{\top},v}^{\top}\mathbbm{E}\big[(\mathbf{I}_{d}\otimes\mathbf{U}_{\bm{\mu},p,l_{p}})\textnormal{vec}(\bm{\Pi}_{t}^{\ast\top})\big]\Big\},

which are essentially
T^{-1/2}d^{a/2}\mathbf{B}^{\top}\mathbf{V}
and
T^{-1/2}d^{a/2}\bm{\Xi}\mathbf{Y}_{W}
at the population level, respectively.

For the rest of the proof of Theorem
1
, we find the rate of
D_{2}
, followed by constructing the asymptotic normality of the dominating term in the expansion of
D_{1}
. For
D_{2}
, we further decompose
D_{2}=F_{1}+F_{2}-F_{3}
where

\displaystyle F_{1}

\displaystyle=[(\mathbf{H}_{20}-\mathbf{H}_{10})^{\top}(\mathbf{H}_{20}-\mathbf{H}_{10})]^{-1}\Big\{(\mathbf{H}_{20}-\mathbf{H}_{10})^{\top}(\mathbf{H}_{20}-\mathbf{H}_{10})

\displaystyle\hskip 12.0pt-T^{-1}d^{a}(\mathbf{B}^{\top}\mathbf{V}-\bm{\Xi}\mathbf{Y}_{W})^{\top}(\mathbf{B}^{\top}\mathbf{V}-\bm{\Xi}\mathbf{Y}_{W})\Big\}D_{2},

\displaystyle F_{2}

\displaystyle=[(\mathbf{H}_{20}-\mathbf{H}_{10})^{\top}(\mathbf{H}_{20}-\mathbf{H}_{10})]^{-1}\Big[(T^{-1/2}d^{a/2}\mathbf{B}^{\top}\mathbf{V}-\mathbf{H}_{10})-(T^{-1/2}d^{a/2}\bm{\Xi}\mathbf{Y}_{W}-\mathbf{H}_{20})\Big]^{\top}

\displaystyle\hskip 12.0pt\cdot\bigg\{\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\Big(\mathbf{I}_{d}\otimes(\mathbf{B}_{t}-\bar{\mathbf{B}})\bm{\gamma}\Big)\mathbf{X}_{t}\bigg\}(\mathbf{X}^{\top}\mathbf{B}^{\nu}(\mathbf{B}^{\nu})^{\top}\mathbf{X})^{-1}\mathbf{X}^{\top}\mathbf{B}^{\nu}(\mathbf{B}^{\nu})^{\top}\bm{\epsilon}^{\nu},

\displaystyle F_{3}

\displaystyle=[(\mathbf{H}_{20}-\mathbf{H}_{10})^{\top}(\mathbf{H}_{20}-\mathbf{H}_{10})]^{-1}(\mathbf{H}_{20}-\mathbf{H}_{10})^{\top}

\displaystyle\hskip 12.0pt\cdot\bigg\{\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\Big(\mathbf{I}_{d}\otimes(\mathbf{B}_{t}-\bar{\mathbf{B}})\bm{\gamma}\Big)\mathbf{X}_{t}\bigg\}(\mathbf{X}^{\top}\mathbf{B}^{\nu}(\mathbf{B}^{\nu})^{\top}\mathbf{X})^{-1}\mathbf{X}^{\top}\mathbf{B}^{\nu}(\mathbf{B}^{\nu})^{\top}\bm{\epsilon}^{\nu}.

To bound the L1 norm of
F_{1}
to
F_{3}
, first observe that by Assumptions (R1) and (R4) we have

\displaystyle\sigma^{2}_{L}(\mathbf{H}_{10})

\displaystyle\geq\sigma^{2}_{L}\Bigg(\Big\{\Big(\mathbf{I}_{d}\otimes(\bm{\gamma}^{\top}\otimes\mathbf{I}_{d})\,\mathbbm{E}\big(\mathbf{U}_{\mathbf{x},1,0}(\bm{\beta}^{\ast}\otimes\mathbf{I}_{d})\bm{\Pi}_{t}^{\ast\top}\big)\Big)\vec{\mathbf{W}_{1}^{\top}},

\displaystyle\hskip 12.0pt\dots,\Big(\mathbf{I}_{d}\otimes(\bm{\gamma}^{\top}\otimes\mathbf{I}_{d})\,\mathbbm{E}\big(\mathbf{U}_{\mathbf{x},p,l_{p}}(\bm{\beta}^{\ast}\otimes\mathbf{I}_{d})\bm{\Pi}_{t}^{\ast\top}\big)\Big)\vec{\mathbf{W}_{p}^{\top}}\Big\}\Bigg)

\displaystyle\geq\sigma_{L}^{2}(\mathbf{D}_{W})\cdot\sigma^{2}_{d^{2}}\Bigg(\Big\{\Big(\mathbf{I}_{d}\otimes(\bm{\gamma}^{\top}\otimes\mathbf{I}_{d})\,\mathbbm{E}\big(\mathbf{U}_{\mathbf{x},1,0}(\bm{\beta}^{\ast}\otimes\mathbf{I}_{d})\bm{\Pi}_{t}^{\ast\top}\big)\Big),

\displaystyle\hskip 12.0pt\dots,\Big(\mathbf{I}_{d}\otimes(\bm{\gamma}^{\top}\otimes\mathbf{I}_{d})\,\mathbbm{E}\big(\mathbf{U}_{\mathbf{x},p,l_{p}}(\bm{\beta}^{\ast}\otimes\mathbf{I}_{d})\bm{\Pi}_{t}^{\ast\top}\big)\Big)\Big\}\Bigg)\geq Cd\cdot d^{a}=Cd^{1+a},

where
C>0
is a generic constant. Similarly, by Assumptions (R1), (R3), (R5) and (R6),

\displaystyle\sigma_{L}(\mathbf{H}_{20})

\displaystyle\geq\sigma_{r}\Big(\mathbbm{E}(\mathbf{X}_{t}\otimes\mathbf{B}_{t}\bm{\gamma})\Big)\,\sigma_{r}\Big(\big[\mathbbm{E}(\mathbf{X}_{t}^{\top}\mathbf{B}_{t})\mathbbm{E}(\mathbf{B}_{t}^{\top}\mathbf{X}_{t})\big]^{-1}\Big)\,\sigma_{r}\Big(\mathbbm{E}(\mathbf{X}_{t}^{\top}\mathbf{B}_{t})\Big)

\displaystyle\hskip 12.0pt\cdot\sigma_{\text{min}}\Big(\Big\{\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{W}_{1}^{\top},r}^{\top}\mathbbm{E}\big[(\mathbf{I}_{d}\otimes\mathbf{U}_{\mathbf{x},1,0})\mathbf{V}_{\bm{\Pi}_{t}^{\ast},r}\big]\bm{\beta}^{\ast},\dots,\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{W}_{p}^{\top},r}^{\top}\mathbbm{E}\big[(\mathbf{I}_{d}\otimes\mathbf{U}_{\mathbf{x},p,l_{p}})\mathbf{V}_{\bm{\Pi}_{t}^{\ast},r}\big]\bm{\beta}^{\ast}\Big\}\Big)

\displaystyle\geq\frac{Cd^{1+a}\cdot d}{\lambda_{\text{max}}[\mathbbm{E}(\mathbf{X}_{t}^{\top}\mathbf{B}_{t})\mathbbm{E}(\mathbf{B}_{t}^{\top}\mathbf{X}_{t})]}\cdot\sigma_{v}[\mathbbm{E}(\ddot{\mathbf{G}})]\cdot\sigma_{L}(\mathbf{D}_{W})

\displaystyle\geq\frac{Cd^{1+a}\cdot d\cdot d^{1/2}}{\lambda_{\text{max}}[\mathbbm{E}(\mathbf{X}_{t}^{\top}\mathbf{B}_{t})\mathbbm{E}(\mathbf{B}_{t}^{\top}\mathbf{X}_{t})]}\geq Cd^{1/2+a},

with some arbitrary constant
C>0
. Notice
\mathbf{H}_{20}
has the smallest singular value of order larger then that for
\mathbf{H}_{10}
, so
\sigma_{L}^{2}(\mathbf{H}_{20}-\mathbf{H}_{10})\geq Cd^{1+a}
for some
C>0
. Thus,

\Big\|[(\mathbf{H}_{20}-\mathbf{H}_{10})^{\top}(\mathbf{H}_{20}-\mathbf{H}_{10})]^{-1}\Big\|_{1}\leq\frac{L^{1/2}}{\lambda_{\text{min}}[(\mathbf{H}_{20}-\mathbf{H}_{10})^{\top}(\mathbf{H}_{20}-\mathbf{H}_{10})]}\leq\frac{L^{1/2}}{Cd^{1+a}}.

(S15)

Consider
F_{1}
first and we hence have on
\mathcal{M}
,

\begin{split}\|F_{1}\|_{1}&\leq\frac{L^{1/2}\cdot L}{Cd^{1+a}}\Bigg\{\Big\|(\mathbf{H}_{20}-T^{-1/2}d^{a/2}\bm{\Xi}\mathbf{Y}_{W})+(T^{-1/2}d^{a/2}\mathbf{B}^{\top}\mathbf{V}-\mathbf{H}_{10})\Big\|_{\text{max}}\cdot\big\|\mathbf{H}_{20}-\mathbf{H}_{10}\big\|_{1}\\
&\hskip 6.0pt+\Big\|T^{-1/2}d^{a/2}(\mathbf{B}^{\top}\mathbf{V}-\bm{\Xi}\mathbf{Y}_{W})\Big\|_{\text{max}}\\
&\hskip 12.0pt\cdot\Big\|(\mathbf{H}_{20}-T^{-1/2}d^{a/2}\bm{\Xi}\mathbf{Y}_{W})+(T^{-1/2}d^{a/2}\mathbf{B}^{\top}\mathbf{V}-\mathbf{H}_{10})\Big\|_{1}\Bigg\}\|D_{2}\|_{1}\\
&=O\Big\{\frac{L^{3/2}}{d^{1+a}}[c_{T}\cdot d^{2}+1\cdot(c_{T}d^{2}+c_{T}d^{2})]\Big\}\|D_{2}\|_{1}=O\Big(c_{T}L^{3/2}d^{1-a}\,\|D_{2}\|_{1}\Big),\end{split}

(S16)

where the last line used the following rates to be shown later,

\displaystyle\big\|\mathbf{H}_{20}-T^{-1/2}d^{a/2}\bm{\Xi}\mathbf{Y}_{W}\big\|_{\text{max}}

\displaystyle=O_{P}(c_{T}),

(S17)

\displaystyle\big\|\mathbf{H}_{10}-T^{-1/2}d^{a/2}\mathbf{B}^{\top}\mathbf{V}\big\|_{\text{max}}

\displaystyle=O_{P}(c_{T}).

(S18)

For neat presentation, we define the following terms whose norms will be bounded and involved in (
S17
) and later,

\displaystyle\mathbf{A}_{1}:=\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{X}_{t}\otimes(\mathbf{B}_{t}-\bar{\mathbf{B}})\bm{\gamma},\hskip 32.0pt\mathbf{A}_{1}^{0}:=\mathbbm{E}(\mathbf{X}_{t}\otimes\mathbf{B}_{t}\bm{\gamma}),

\displaystyle\mathbf{A}_{2}:=\Big(\frac{1}{T}\mathbf{X}^{\top}\mathbf{B}^{\nu}\frac{1}{T}(\mathbf{B}^{\nu})^{\top}\mathbf{X}\Big)^{-1},\hskip 14.0pt\mathbf{A}_{2}^{0}:=\big[\mathbbm{E}(\mathbf{X}_{t}^{\top}\mathbf{B}_{t})\mathbbm{E}(\mathbf{B}_{t}^{\top}\mathbf{X}_{t})\big]^{-1},

\displaystyle\mathbf{A}_{3}:=\frac{1}{T}\mathbf{X}^{\top}\mathbf{B}^{\nu},\hskip 89.0pt\mathbf{A}_{3}^{0}:=\mathbbm{E}(\mathbf{X}_{t}^{\top}\mathbf{B}_{t}),

\displaystyle\mathbf{A}_{4,j,k}:=\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}z_{j,k,t}(\mathbf{B}_{t}-\bar{\mathbf{B}})^{\top}\mathbf{W}_{j}(\bm{\Pi}_{t}^{\ast}\bm{\mu}^{\ast}+\bm{\Pi}_{t}^{\ast}\mathbf{X}_{t}\bm{\beta}^{\ast}),

\displaystyle\mathbf{A}_{4,j,k}^{0}:=\mathbbm{E}(\mathbf{A}_{4,j,k}),\hskip 74.0pt\mathbf{A}_{5,j,k}:=\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}z_{j,k,t}(\mathbf{B}_{t}-\bar{\mathbf{B}})^{\top}\mathbf{W}_{j}\bm{\Pi}_{t}^{\ast}\bm{\epsilon}_{t}.

On
\mathcal{M}
, we immediately have from Lemma
2
(using
\mathcal{A}_{1},\mathcal{A}_{4},\mathcal{A}_{6}
),

\big\|\mathbf{A}_{1}-\mathbf{A}_{1}^{0}\big\|_{\text{max}}=O\Big(c_{T}+c_{T}(c_{T}+\mu_{b,\text{max}})\Big)=O(c_{T}),

(S19)

which also gives
\big\|\mathbf{A}_{1}\big\|_{\text{max}}\leq\big\|\mathbf{A}_{1}^{0}\big\|_{\text{max}}+\big\|\mathbf{A}_{1}-\mathbf{A}_{1}^{0}\big\|_{\text{max}}=O(1+c_{T})=O(1)
. Hence with Assumptions (R5) and (R10), we also have on
\mathcal{M}
that

\big\|\mathbf{A}_{1}\big\|_{1}\leq\big\|\mathbf{A}_{1}^{0}\big\|_{1}+\big\|\mathbf{A}_{1}-\mathbf{A}_{1}^{0}\big\|_{1}=O(d^{1+a}+c_{T}d^{2})=O(d^{1+a}).

(S20)

Similarly, with Lemma
2
(using
\mathcal{A}_{1}
) and Assumption (R3) we have on
\mathcal{M}
,

\big\|\mathbf{A}_{3}-\mathbf{A}_{3}^{0}\big\|_{1}=O(c_{T}d),\,\,\,\big\|\mathbf{A}_{3}^{0}\big\|_{1}=O(d),\,\,\,\big\|\mathbf{A}_{3}\big\|_{1}\leq\big\|\mathbf{A}_{3}-\mathbf{A}_{3}^{0}\big\|_{1}+\big\|\mathbf{A}_{3}^{0}\big\|_{1}=O(d).

(S21)

Rewrite
\mathbf{A}_{2}^{0}=(\mathbf{A}_{3}^{0}\mathbf{A}_{3}^{0\top})^{-1}
and
\mathbf{A}_{2}=(\mathbf{A}_{3}\mathbf{A}_{3}^{\top})^{-1}
, by Assumption (R3),

\big\|\mathbf{A}_{2}^{0}\big\|_{1}\leq\frac{r^{1/2}}{\lambda_{\text{min}}(\mathbf{A}_{3}^{0}\mathbf{A}_{3}^{0\top})}=O(d^{-2}).

(S22)

Moreover, from (
S21
) we have on
\mathcal{M}

\displaystyle\big\|(\mathbf{A}_{2}^{0})^{-1}-\mathbf{A}_{2}^{-1}\big\|_{1}

\displaystyle\leq\big\|\mathbf{A}_{3}^{0}\mathbf{A}_{3}^{0\top}-\mathbf{A}_{3}\mathbf{A}_{3}^{\top}\big\|_{1}\leq\big\|\mathbf{A}_{3}^{0}-\mathbf{A}_{3}\big\|_{1}\big\|\mathbf{A}_{3}^{0\top}\big\|_{1}+\big\|\mathbf{A}_{3}\big\|_{1}\big\|\mathbf{A}_{3}^{0\top}-\mathbf{A}_{3}^{\top}\big\|_{1}

\displaystyle=O(c_{T}d\cdot d+d\cdot c_{T}d)=O(c_{T}d^{2}).

Thus, rewriting
\mathbf{A}_{2}-\mathbf{A}_{2}^{0}=(\mathbf{A}_{2}-\mathbf{A}_{2}^{0})[(\mathbf{A}_{2}^{0})^{-1}-\mathbf{A}_{2}^{-1}]\mathbf{A}_{2}^{0}+\mathbf{A}_{2}^{0}[(\mathbf{A}_{2}^{0})^{-1}-\mathbf{A}_{2}^{-1}]\mathbf{A}_{2}^{0}
and we have on
\mathcal{M}
,

\big\|\mathbf{A}_{2}-\mathbf{A}_{2}^{0}\big\|_{1}=o\Big(\big\|\mathbf{A}_{2}-\mathbf{A}_{2}^{0}\big\|_{1}\Big)+O(c_{T}d^{2}\cdot d^{-4})=O(c_{T}d^{-2}).

(S23)

Consider now
\mathbf{A}_{4,j,k}^{0}
for any
j\in[p],k\in[l_{j}]\cup\{0\}
. First, we have on
\mathcal{M}
,

\displaystyle\hskip 12.0pt\Big\|\mathbbm{E}\Big(\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}z_{j,k,t}(\mathbf{B}_{t}-\bar{\mathbf{B}})^{\top}\mathbf{W}_{j}\bm{\Pi}_{t}^{\ast}\bm{\mu}^{\ast}\Big)\Big\|_{1}\leq\Big\|\mathbbm{E}\Big(\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}z_{j,k,t}(\mathbf{B}_{t}-\bar{\mathbf{B}})^{\top}\Big)\Big\|_{\text{max}}\big\|\mathbf{W}_{j}\big\|_{1}\big\|\bm{\Pi}_{t}^{\ast}\bm{\mu}^{\ast}\big\|_{1}

\displaystyle=O\Big(d\cdot\big\|\bm{\Pi}_{t}^{\ast}\big\|_{\infty}\big\|\bm{\mu}^{\ast}\big\|_{\text{max}}\Big)=O(d),

where the last equality used Assumption (R1). Thus on
\mathcal{M}
,

\begin{split}&\hskip 12.0pt\big\|\mathbf{A}_{4,j,k}^{0}\big\|_{1}\leq\big\|\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{W}_{j}^{\top},v}^{\top}\big[\mathbf{I}_{d}\otimes\mathbbm{E}(\mathbf{U}_{\mathbf{x},j,k})\big]\mathbf{V}_{\bm{\Pi}_{t}^{\ast},r}\bm{\beta}^{\ast}\big\|_{1}+\Big\|\mathbbm{E}\Big(\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}z_{j,k,t}(\mathbf{B}_{t}-\bar{\mathbf{B}})^{\top}\mathbf{W}_{j}\bm{\Pi}_{t}^{\ast}\bm{\mu}^{\ast}\Big)\Big\|_{1}\\
&\leq\big\|\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{W}_{j}^{\top},v}^{\top}\big[\mathbf{I}_{d}\otimes\mathbbm{E}(\mathbf{U}_{\mathbf{x},j,k})\big]\big\|_{\text{max}}\big\|\mathbf{V}_{\bm{\Pi}_{t}^{\ast},r}\big\|_{1}\big\|\bm{\beta}^{\ast}\big\|_{1}+O(d)\\
&\leq\max_{i,n\in[d]}\max_{m\in[v]}\max_{q\in[r]}\mathbf{W}_{j,\cdot i}^{\top}\mathbbm{E}\Big\{\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}z_{j,k,t}(\mathbf{B}_{t}-\bar{\mathbf{B}})_{\cdot m}X_{t,nq}\Big\}\big\|\mathbf{V}_{\bm{\Pi}_{t}^{\ast},r}\big\|_{1}\big\|\bm{\beta}^{\ast}\big\|_{1}+O(d)=O(d),\end{split}

(S24)

where the last equality used Assumption (R1). Furthermore, we have on
\mathcal{M}
that

\begin{split}&\hskip 12.0pt\big\|\mathbf{A}_{4,j,k}-\mathbf{A}_{4,j,k}^{0}\big\|_{1}\\
&\leq\Big\|\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}z_{j,k,t}(\mathbf{B}_{t}-\bar{\mathbf{B}})^{\top}-\mathbbm{E}\Big\{\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}z_{j,k,t}(\mathbf{B}_{t}-\bar{\mathbf{B}})^{\top}\Big\}\Big\|_{\text{max}}\big\|\mathbf{W}_{j}\big\|_{1}\big\|\bm{\Pi}_{t}^{\ast}\bm{\mu}^{\ast}\big\|_{1}\\
&\hskip 12.0pt+\big\|\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{W}_{j}^{\top},v}^{\top}\big\{\mathbf{I}_{d}\otimes[\mathbf{U}_{\mathbf{x},j,k}-\mathbbm{E}(\mathbf{U}_{\mathbf{x},j,k})]\big\}\mathbf{V}_{\bm{\Pi}_{t}^{\ast},r}\bm{\beta}^{\ast}\big\|_{1}\\
&=O\Big([c_{T}+1\cdot c_{T}+1\cdot c_{T}+1\cdot(c_{T}\vee 0)]\,d\Big)+O\Big(\big\|\mathbf{V}_{\bm{\Pi}_{t}^{\ast},r}\big\|_{1}\big\|\bm{\beta}^{\ast}\big\|_{1}\Big)\\
&\hskip 12.0pt\cdot\big\|\mathbf{W}_{j}^{\top}\big\|_{1}\max_{n,m\in[d]}\max_{s\in[v]}\max_{q\in[r]}\Big|\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}z_{j,k,t}(\mathbf{B}_{t}-\bar{\mathbf{B}})_{ms}X_{t,nq}-\mathbbm{E}\Big(\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}z_{j,k,t}(\mathbf{B}_{t}-\bar{\mathbf{B}})_{ms}X_{t,nq}\Big)\Big|\\
&=O(c_{T}d),\end{split}

(S25)

where the second last equality used Assumption (R1) and
\mathcal{A}_{4},\,\mathcal{A}_{12},\,\mathcal{A}_{13}
, while the last used
\mathcal{A}_{4},\,\mathcal{A}_{8},\,\mathcal{A}_{9}
. In particular, we used the following as an immediate result of
\mathcal{A}_{13}
,

\max_{m\in[p]}\max_{n\in[l_{m}]\cup\{0\}}\bigg|\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}z_{m,n,t}-\mathbbm{E}\Big\{\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}z_{m,n,t}\Big\}\bigg|\leq c_{T}\vee 0.

Lastly for any
j\in[p],k\in[l_{j}]\cup\{0\}
, similar to (
S10
), we have on
\mathcal{M}
that

\begin{split}\big\|\mathbf{A}_{5,j,k}\big\|_{1}&=\big\|\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{W}_{j}^{\top},v}^{\top}\big(\mathbf{I}_{d}\otimes\mathbf{U}_{\bm{\epsilon},j,k}\big)\mathbf{V}_{\bm{\Pi}_{t}^{\ast},r}\big\|_{1}\leq\big\|\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{W}_{j}^{\top},v}^{\top}\big(\mathbf{I}_{d}\otimes\mathbf{U}_{\bm{\epsilon},j,k}\big)\big\|_{\text{max}}\big\|\mathbf{V}_{\bm{\Pi}_{t}^{\ast},r}\big\|_{1}\\
&\leq\big\|\mathbf{W}_{j}^{\top}\big\|_{1}\max_{m,n\in[d]}\max_{s\in[v]}\Big|\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}z_{j,k,t}(\mathbf{B}_{t}-\bar{\mathbf{B}})_{ms}\epsilon_{t,n}\Big|\cdot\big\|\mathbf{V}_{\bm{\Pi}_{t}^{\ast},r}\big\|_{1}=O(c_{T}d).\end{split}

(S26)

Consider now (
S17
), we have

\displaystyle\hskip 12.0pt\Big\|\mathbf{H}_{20}-T^{-1/2}d^{a/2}\bm{\Xi}\mathbf{Y}_{W}\Big\|_{\text{max}}=\max_{j,k}\Big\|\mathbf{A}_{1}\mathbf{A}_{2}\mathbf{A}_{3}(\mathbf{A}_{4,j,k}+\mathbf{A}_{5,j,k})-\mathbf{A}_{1}^{0}\mathbf{A}_{2}^{0}\mathbf{A}_{3}^{0}\mathbf{A}_{4,j,k}^{0}\Big\|_{\text{max}}

\displaystyle\leq\max_{j,k}\big\|\mathbf{A}_{1}\big\|_{\text{max}}\big\|\mathbf{A}_{2}\big\|_{1}\big\|\mathbf{A}_{3}\big\|_{1}\big\|\mathbf{A}_{5,j,k}\big\|_{1}

\displaystyle\hskip 12.0pt+\max_{j,k}\Big\{\big\|\mathbf{A}_{1}\big\|_{\text{max}}\big\|\mathbf{A}_{2}\mathbf{A}_{3}\mathbf{A}_{4,j,k}-\mathbf{A}_{2}^{0}\mathbf{A}_{3}^{0}\mathbf{A}_{4,j,k}^{0}\big\|_{1}+\big\|\mathbf{A}_{1}-\mathbf{A}_{1}^{0}\big\|_{\text{max}}\big\|\mathbf{A}_{2}^{0}\mathbf{A}_{3}^{0}\mathbf{A}_{4,j,k}^{0}\big\|_{1}\Big\},\,\,\,\text{with}

\displaystyle\max_{j,k}\big\|\mathbf{A}_{2}\mathbf{A}_{3}\mathbf{A}_{4,j,k}-\mathbf{A}_{2}^{0}\mathbf{A}_{3}^{0}\mathbf{A}_{4,j,k}^{0}\big\|_{1}\leq\max_{j,k}\Big\{\big\|\mathbf{A}_{2}\big\|_{1}\big\|\mathbf{A}_{3}-\mathbf{A}_{3}^{0}\big\|_{1}\big\|\mathbf{A}_{4,j,k}\big\|_{1}

\displaystyle\hskip 120.0pt+\big\|\mathbf{A}_{2}\big\|_{1}\big\|\mathbf{A}_{3}^{0}\big\|_{1}\big\|\mathbf{A}_{4,j,k}-\mathbf{A}_{4,j,k}^{0}\big\|_{1}+\big\|\mathbf{A}_{2}-\mathbf{A}_{2}^{0}\big\|_{1}\big\|\mathbf{A}_{3}^{0}\big\|_{1}\big\|\mathbf{A}_{4,j,k}^{0}\big\|_{1}\Big\}.

Together with all the rates from (

) to (

), we have (
S17
) true on
\mathcal{M}
.

For (
S18
), consider for any
j\in[p],k\in[l_{j}]\cup\{0\}
, we have on
\mathcal{M}
that

\begin{split}&\hskip 12.0pt\Big\|\Big[\mathbf{I}_{d}\otimes(\bm{\gamma}^{\top}\otimes\mathbf{I}_{d})\mathbf{U}_{\bm{\epsilon},j,k}\bm{\Pi}_{t}^{\ast\top}\Big]\vec{\mathbf{W}_{j}^{\top}}\Big\|_{\text{max}}=\max_{i\in[d]}\big\|(\bm{\gamma}^{\top}\otimes\mathbf{I}_{d})\mathbf{U}_{\bm{\epsilon},j,k}\bm{\Pi}_{t}^{\ast\top}\mathbf{W}_{j,i\cdot}\big\|_{\text{max}}\\
&\leq\max_{i\in[d]}\big\|(\bm{\gamma}^{\top}\otimes\mathbf{I}_{d})\mathbf{U}_{\bm{\epsilon},j,k}\big\|_{\text{max}}\big\|\bm{\Pi}_{t}^{\ast}\big\|_{\infty}\big\|\mathbf{W}_{j,i\cdot}\big\|_{1}\\
&=O(1)\cdot\|\bm{\gamma}\|_{1}\cdot\max_{m,n\in[d]}\max_{s\in[v]}\Big|\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}z_{j,k,t}(\mathbf{B}_{t}-\bar{\mathbf{B}})_{ms}\epsilon_{t,n}\Big|=O(c_{T}),\end{split}

(S27)

where the second last equality used Assumption (R1) and the result below (
S8
), and the last is similar to (
S10
). In a similar way on
\mathcal{M}
,

\begin{split}&\hskip 12.0pt\Big\|\Big[\mathbf{I}_{d}\otimes(\bm{\gamma}^{\top}\otimes\mathbf{I}_{d})\big(\mathbf{U}_{\bm{\mu},j,k}-\mathbbm{E}(\mathbf{U}_{\bm{\mu},j,k})\big)\bm{\Pi}_{t}^{\ast\top}\Big]\vec{\mathbf{W}_{j}^{\top}}\Big\|_{\text{max}}\\
&\leq\max_{i\in[d]}\big\|(\bm{\gamma}^{\top}\otimes\mathbf{I}_{d})\big(\mathbf{U}_{\bm{\mu},j,k}-\mathbbm{E}(\mathbf{U}_{\bm{\mu},j,k})\big)\big\|_{\text{max}}\big\|\bm{\Pi}_{t}^{\ast}\big\|_{\infty}\big\|\mathbf{W}_{j,i\cdot}\big\|_{1}\\
&=O\Big(\|\bm{\gamma}\|_{1}\|\bm{\mu}^{\ast}\|_{\text{max}}\Big)\cdot\Big\|\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}z_{j,k,t}(\mathbf{B}_{t}-\bar{\mathbf{B}})^{\top}-\mathbbm{E}\Big\{\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}z_{j,k,t}(\mathbf{B}_{t}-\bar{\mathbf{B}})^{\top}\Big\}\Big\|_{\text{max}}=O(c_{T}),\end{split}

(S28)

with the last line similar to (
S25
) which is also involved in the last line of the following,

\begin{split}&\hskip 12.0pt\Big\|\Big[\mathbf{I}_{d}\otimes(\bm{\gamma}^{\top}\otimes\mathbf{I}_{d})\big(\mathbf{U}_{\mathbf{x},j,k}-\mathbbm{E}(\mathbf{U}_{\mathbf{x},j,k})\big)(\bm{\beta}^{\ast}\otimes\mathbf{I}_{d})\bm{\Pi}_{t}^{\ast\top}\Big]\vec{\mathbf{W}_{j}^{\top}}\Big\|_{\text{max}}\\
&\leq\max_{i\in[d]}\big\|(\bm{\gamma}^{\top}\otimes\mathbf{I}_{d})\big(\mathbf{U}_{\mathbf{x},j,k}-\mathbbm{E}(\mathbf{U}_{\mathbf{x},j,k})\big)\big\|_{\text{max}}\big\|\bm{\beta}^{\ast}\otimes\mathbf{I}_{d}\big\|_{1}\big\|\bm{\Pi}_{t}^{\ast}\big\|_{\infty}\big\|\mathbf{W}_{j,i\cdot}\big\|_{1}\\
&=O\Big(\|\bm{\gamma}\|_{1}\Big)\,\max_{m,n\in[d]}\max_{s\in[v]}\max_{q\in[r]}\Big|\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}z_{j,k,t}(\mathbf{B}_{t}-\bar{\mathbf{B}})_{ms}X_{t,nq}-\mathbbm{E}\Big(\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}z_{j,k,t}(\mathbf{B}_{t}-\bar{\mathbf{B}})_{ms}X_{t,nq}\Big)\Big|\\
&=O(c_{T}).\end{split}

(S29)

Combining (
S27
), (
S28
) and (
S29
), we have (
S18
) true by the following decomposition,

\displaystyle\hskip 12.0pt\Big\|\mathbf{H}_{10}-T^{-1/2}d^{a/2}\mathbf{B}^{\top}\mathbf{V}\Big\|_{\text{max}}

\displaystyle\leq\max_{j,k}\Big\|\Big[\mathbf{I}_{d}\otimes(\bm{\gamma}^{\top}\otimes\mathbf{I}_{d})\mathbf{U}_{\bm{\epsilon},j,k}\bm{\Pi}_{t}^{\ast\top}\Big]\vec{\mathbf{W}_{j}^{\top}}\Big\|_{\text{max}}

\displaystyle\hskip 12.0pt+\max_{j,k}\Big\|\Big[\mathbf{I}_{d}\otimes(\bm{\gamma}^{\top}\otimes\mathbf{I}_{d})\big(\mathbf{U}_{\bm{\mu},j,k}-\mathbbm{E}(\mathbf{U}_{\bm{\mu},j,k})\big)\bm{\Pi}_{t}^{\ast\top}\Big]\vec{\mathbf{W}_{j}^{\top}}\Big\|_{\text{max}}

\displaystyle\hskip 12.0pt+\max_{j,k}\Big\|\Big[\mathbf{I}_{d}\otimes(\bm{\gamma}^{\top}\otimes\mathbf{I}_{d})\big(\mathbf{U}_{\mathbf{x},j,k}-\mathbbm{E}(\mathbf{U}_{\mathbf{x},j,k})\big)(\bm{\beta}^{\ast}\otimes\mathbf{I}_{d})\bm{\Pi}_{t}^{\ast\top}\Big]\vec{\mathbf{W}_{j}^{\top}}\Big\|_{\text{max}}.

Next for
F_{2}
and
F_{3}
, we consider first on
\mathcal{M}
,

\begin{split}&\hskip 12.0pt\Big\|\Big\{\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\Big(\mathbf{I}_{d}\otimes(\mathbf{B}_{t}-\bar{\mathbf{B}})\bm{\gamma}\Big)\mathbf{X}_{t}\Big\}(\mathbf{X}^{\top}\mathbf{B}^{\nu}(\mathbf{B}^{\nu})^{\top}\mathbf{X})^{-1}\mathbf{X}^{\top}\mathbf{B}^{\nu}(\mathbf{B}^{\nu})^{\top}\bm{\epsilon}^{\nu}\Big\|_{1}\\
&=\bigg\|\mathbf{A}_{1}\mathbf{A}_{2}\mathbf{A}_{3}\,\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}(\mathbf{B}_{t}-\bar{\mathbf{B}})^{\top}\bm{\epsilon}_{t}\bigg\|_{1}\leq\big\|\mathbf{A}_{1}\big\|_{1}\big\|\mathbf{A}_{2}\big\|_{1}\big\|\mathbf{A}_{3}\big\|_{1}\cdot v\max_{j\in[r]}\bigg|\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\sum_{q=1}^{d}B_{t,qj}\epsilon_{t,q}\bigg|\\
&=O\big(d^{1+a}\cdot d^{-2}\cdot d\cdot c_{T}d^{\frac{1}{2}+\frac{1}{2w}}\big)=O\big(c_{T}d^{\frac{1}{2}+\frac{1}{2w}+a}\big),\end{split}

(S30)

where the first equality used the fact that
\mathbf{X}_{t}=\mathbf{X}_{t}\otimes 1
, the second last used (
S20
), (
S21
), (
S22
) and
\mathcal{A}_{3}
in Lemma
2
. Then for
F_{2}
, we have on
\mathcal{M}
that

\begin{split}\|F_{2}\|_{1}&\leq\frac{L^{1/2}\cdot L}{Cd^{1+a}}\Big(\Big\|(\mathbf{H}_{20}-T^{-1/2}d^{a/2}\bm{\Xi}\mathbf{Y}_{W})\Big\|_{\text{max}}+\Big\|(T^{-1/2}d^{a/2}\mathbf{B}^{\top}\mathbf{V}-\mathbf{H}_{10})\Big\|_{\text{max}}\Big)\\
&\hskip 12.0pt\cdot\Big\|\Big\{\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\Big(\mathbf{I}_{d}\otimes(\mathbf{B}_{t}-\bar{\mathbf{B}})\bm{\gamma}\Big)\mathbf{X}_{t}\Big\}(\mathbf{X}^{\top}\mathbf{B}^{\nu}(\mathbf{B}^{\nu})^{\top}\mathbf{X})^{-1}\mathbf{X}^{\top}\mathbf{B}^{\nu}(\mathbf{B}^{\nu})^{\top}\bm{\epsilon}^{\nu}\Big\|_{1}\\
&=O\big(L^{3/2}\cdot d^{-1-a}\cdot c_{T}\cdot c_{T}d^{\frac{1}{2}+\frac{1}{2w}+a}\big)=O\big(c_{T}^{2}L^{3/2}d^{-\frac{1}{2}+\frac{1}{2w}}\big),\end{split}

(S31)

where the last line used (
S15
), (
S17
), (
S18
) and (
S30
). Similarly, we have
\|F_{3}\|_{1}=O\big(c_{T}L^{3/2}d^{-\frac{1}{2}+\frac{1}{2w}}\big)
on
\mathcal{M}
, and hence together with
L=O(1)
, (
S16
) and (
S31
), it holds on
\mathcal{M}
that

\big\|D_{2}\big\|_{1}\leq\big\|F_{1}\big\|_{1}+\big\|F_{2}\big\|_{1}+\big\|F_{3}\big\|_{1}=O\big(c_{T}d^{-\frac{1}{2}+\frac{1}{2w}}\big).

(S32)

Similar to the way we decompose
D_{2}
, we can rewrite
D_{1}=F_{4}+F_{5}-F_{6}
where

\displaystyle F_{4}

\displaystyle=[(\mathbf{H}_{20}-\mathbf{H}_{10})^{\top}(\mathbf{H}_{20}-\mathbf{H}_{10})]^{-1}\Big\{(\mathbf{H}_{20}-\mathbf{H}_{10})^{\top}(\mathbf{H}_{20}-\mathbf{H}_{10})

\displaystyle\hskip 12.0pt-T^{-1}d^{a}(\mathbf{B}^{\top}\mathbf{V}-\bm{\Xi}\mathbf{Y}_{W})^{\top}(\mathbf{B}^{\top}\mathbf{V}-\bm{\Xi}\mathbf{Y}_{W})\Big\}D_{1},

\displaystyle F_{5}

\displaystyle=[(\mathbf{H}_{20}-\mathbf{H}_{10})^{\top}(\mathbf{H}_{20}-\mathbf{H}_{10})]^{-1}\Big[(T^{-1/2}d^{a/2}\mathbf{B}^{\top}\mathbf{V}-\mathbf{H}_{10})-(T^{-1/2}d^{a/2}\bm{\Xi}\mathbf{Y}_{W}-\mathbf{H}_{20})\Big]^{\top}

\displaystyle\hskip 12.0pt\cdot T^{-1/2}d^{a/2}\mathbf{B}^{\top}\bm{\epsilon},

\displaystyle F_{6}

\displaystyle=[(\mathbf{H}_{20}-\mathbf{H}_{10})^{\top}(\mathbf{H}_{20}-\mathbf{H}_{10})]^{-1}(\mathbf{H}_{20}-\mathbf{H}_{10})^{\top}\cdot T^{-1/2}d^{a/2}\mathbf{B}^{\top}\bm{\epsilon}.

From (
S16
), it is direct that
F_{4}=O\big(c_{T}L^{3/2}d^{1-a}\,\|D_{1}\|_{1}\big)
on
\mathcal{M}
. Moreover, (
S17
) and (
S18
) imply that
F_{5}
has a smaller rate than that of
F_{6}
. Given
L=O(1)
, we next construct the asymptotic normality of
\bm{\alpha}^{\top}F_{6}
for any given non-zero
\bm{\alpha}\in\mathbbm{R}^{L}
with
\|\bm{\alpha}\|_{1}\leq c<\infty
.

Denote by
\mathbf{R}_{1}:=[(\mathbf{H}_{20}-\mathbf{H}_{10})^{\top}(\mathbf{H}_{20}-\mathbf{H}_{10})]^{-1}(\mathbf{H}_{20}-\mathbf{H}_{10})^{\top}
, we have

\displaystyle\bm{\alpha}^{\top}F_{6}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{T}\bm{\alpha}^{\top}\mathbf{R}_{1}\Big(\mathbf{I}_{d}\otimes\big\{(\mathbf{B}_{1}-\bar{\mathbf{B}},\dots,\mathbf{B}_{T}-\bar{\mathbf{B}})(\mathbf{I}_{T}\otimes\bm{\gamma})\big\}\Big)\text{vec}\big((\bm{\epsilon}_{1},\dots,\bm{\epsilon}_{T})^{\top}\big)

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{T}\bm{\alpha}^{\top}\mathbf{R}_{1}\Big(\mathbf{I}_{d}\otimes\big\{(\mathbf{B}_{1}-\bar{\mathbf{B}})\bm{\gamma},\dots,(\mathbf{B}_{T}-\bar{\mathbf{B}})\bm{\gamma}\big\}\Big)\sum_{t=1}^{T}\Big\{\bm{\epsilon}_{t}\otimes\big(\mathbbm{1}_{\{j=t\}}\big)_{j\in[T]}\Big\}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\bm{\alpha}^{\top}\mathbf{R}_{1}\Big(\bm{\epsilon}_{t}\otimes(\mathbf{B}_{t}-\bar{\mathbf{B}})\bm{\gamma}\Big)=\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\bm{\alpha}^{\top}\mathbf{R}_{1}\Big(\bm{\epsilon}_{t}\otimes[\mathbf{B}_{t}-\mathbbm{E}(\mathbf{B}_{t})]\bm{\gamma}(1+o_{P}(1))\Big),

where the vector
\big(\mathbbm{1}_{\{j=t\}}\big)_{j\in[T]}\in\mathbbm{R}^{T}
has value
\mathbbm{1}_{\{j=t\}}
at each
j
-th entry, and the last equality used
\mathcal{A}_{4}
in Lemma
2
. Hence to apply Theorem 3 (ii) of
Wu (2011)
, we need to show

\sum_{t\geq 0}\Big\|P_{0}\Big\{\bm{\alpha}^{\top}\mathbf{R}_{1}\big(\bm{\epsilon}_{t}\otimes[\mathbf{B}_{t}-\mathbbm{E}(\mathbf{B}_{t})]\bm{\gamma}\big)\Big\}\Big\|_{2}<\infty,

(S33)

where
P_{0}(\cdot):=\mathbbm{E}_{0}(\cdot)-\mathbbm{E}_{-1}(\cdot)
and
\mathbbm{E}_{i}(\cdot):=\mathbbm{E}(\cdot\mid\sigma(\mathcal{G}_{i},\mathcal{H}_{i}))
. Notice that

\displaystyle\hskip 12.0pt\Big\|P_{0}\Big\{\bm{\alpha}^{\top}\mathbf{R}_{1}\big(\bm{\epsilon}_{t}\otimes[\mathbf{B}_{t}-\mathbbm{E}(\mathbf{B}_{t})]\bm{\gamma}\big)\Big\}\Big\|_{2}

\displaystyle=\Big\|\bm{\alpha}^{\top}\mathbf{R}_{1}\Big\{P_{0}(\bm{\epsilon}_{t})\otimes\mathbbm{E}_{0}([\mathbf{B}_{t}-\mathbbm{E}(\mathbf{B}_{t})]\bm{\gamma})\Big\}+\bm{\alpha}^{\top}\mathbf{R}_{1}\Big\{\mathbbm{E}_{-1}(\bm{\epsilon}_{t})\otimes P_{0}([\mathbf{B}_{t}-\mathbbm{E}(\mathbf{B}_{t})]\bm{\gamma})\Big\}\Big\|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq\Big\{2\bm{\alpha}^{\top}\mathbf{R}_{1}\Big\{\mathbbm{E}\big(P_{0}(\bm{\epsilon}_{t})\,P_{0}(\bm{\epsilon}_{t})^{\top}\big)\otimes\mathbbm{E}\big(\mathbbm{E}_{0}([\mathbf{B}_{t}-\mathbbm{E}(\mathbf{B}_{t})]\bm{\gamma})\,\mathbbm{E}_{0}(\bm{\gamma}^{\top}[\mathbf{B}_{t}-\mathbbm{E}(\mathbf{B}_{t})]^{\top})\big)\Big\}\mathbf{R}_{1}^{\top}\bm{\alpha}\Big\}^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle+\Big(2\bm{\alpha}^{\top}\mathbf{R}_{1}\Big\{\mathbbm{E}\big(\mathbbm{E}_{-1}(\bm{\epsilon}_{t})\,\mathbbm{E}_{-1}(\bm{\epsilon}_{t})^{\top}\big)\otimes\mathbbm{E}\big(P_{0}([\mathbf{B}_{t}-\mathbbm{E}(\mathbf{B}_{t})]\bm{\gamma})\,P_{0}(\bm{\gamma}^{\top}[\mathbf{B}_{t}-\mathbbm{E}(\mathbf{B}_{t})]^{\top})\big)\Big\}\mathbf{R}_{1}^{\top}\bm{\alpha}\Big)^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle=O\Big(\big\|\bm{\alpha}\big\|_{1}\big\|\mathbf{R}_{1}\big\|_{\infty}\Big)\cdot\Big(\max_{j\in[d]}\big\|P_{0}(\epsilon_{t,j})\big\|_{2}\cdot\max_{j\in[d]}\text{Var}^{1/2}(\mathbf{B}_{t,j\cdot}^{\top}\bm{\gamma})+\sigma_{\text{max}}\max_{j\in[d]}\big\|P_{0}(\mathbf{B}_{t,j\cdot}^{\top}\bm{\gamma})\big\|_{2}\Big)

\displaystyle=O\Big(\max_{j\in[d]}\big\|P_{0}^{\epsilon}(\epsilon_{t,j})\big\|_{2}+\max_{j\in[d]}\max_{k\in[v]}\big\|P_{0}^{b}(B_{t,jk})\big\|_{2}\Big),

where the second last equality used
\text{Var}(\cdot)=\text{Var}(\mathbbm{E}_{i}(\cdot))+\mathbbm{E}(\text{Var}_{i}(\cdot))\geq\text{Var}(\mathbbm{E}_{i}(\cdot))
, and the last used Assumption (R2) and
\big\|\mathbf{R}_{1}\big\|_{\infty}=O(1)
which is implied from (
S15
),
\big\|\mathbf{H}_{10}\big\|_{1}=O(d)
and
\big\|\mathbf{H}_{20}\big\|_{1}=O(d^{1+a})
. With Assumption (R7), (
S33
) is true. Therefore, with definition

s_{1}:=\bm{\alpha}^{\top}\mathbf{R}_{1}\bm{\Sigma}\mathbf{R}_{1}^{\top}\bm{\alpha},\,\,\,\bm{\Sigma}:=\sum_{\tau}\mathbbm{E}(\bm{\epsilon}_{t}\bm{\epsilon}_{t+\tau}^{\top})\otimes\mathbbm{E}\big[(\mathbf{B}_{t}-\mathbbm{E}(\mathbf{B}_{t}))\bm{\gamma}\bm{\gamma}^{\top}(\mathbf{B}_{t+\tau}-\mathbbm{E}(\mathbf{B}_{t}))^{\top}\big],

(S34)

we have by Theorem 3 (ii) of
Wu (2011)
that

T^{1/2}s_{1}^{-1/2}\bm{\alpha}^{\top}F_{6}\xrightarrow{D}\mathcal{N}(0,1).

Then equivalently we have

T^{1/2}(\mathbf{R}_{1}\bm{\Sigma}\mathbf{R}_{1}^{\top})^{-1/2}F_{6}\xrightarrow{D}\mathcal{N}(\mathbf{0},\mathbf{I}_{L}),

(S35)

so that
F_{6}
is at least
T^{1/2}d^{(1+a-b)/2}
-convergent which used
\lambda_{\text{max}}(\mathbf{R}_{1}\mathbf{R}_{1}^{\top})=O(d^{-1-a})
from (
S15
), and all eigenvalues of
d^{-b}\bm{\Sigma}
uniformly bounded from 0 and infinity by Assumption (R8). Hence,
\big\|D_{1}\big\|_{1}=O\big(\|F_{6}\|_{1}\big)=O\big(T^{-1/2}d^{-(1+a-b)/2}\big)
on
\mathcal{M}
, and by (
S32
) we have

\big\|\widetilde{\bm{\phi}}-\bm{\phi}^{\ast}\big\|_{1}=O_{P}\Big(\big\|D_{1}\big\|_{1}+\big\|D_{2}\big\|_{1}\Big)=O_{P}\big(T^{-1/2}d^{-(1+a-b)/2}+c_{T}d^{-\frac{1}{2}+\frac{1}{2w}}\big)=O_{P}\big(c_{T}d^{-\frac{1}{2}+\frac{1}{2w}}\big),

where the last equality used Assumption (R10). With the above plugged into (
S12
), the proof of Theorem
1
is complete.

\square

Proof of Theorem
2
.

By the KKT condition,
\widehat{\bm{\phi}}
is a solution to the adaptive LASSO problem in (
2.15
) if and only if there exists a subgradient

\mathbf{h}=\partial(\mathbf{u}^{\top}|\widehat{\bm{\phi}}|)=\left\{\mathbf{h}\in\mathbbm{R}^{L}:\left\{\begin{array}[]{ll}h_{i}=u_{i}\,\text{sign}(\widehat{\phi}_{i}),&\hbox{$\widehat{\phi}_{i}\neq 0$;}\\
|h_{i}|\leq u_{i},&\hbox{otherwise.}\end{array}\right.\right\},

such that differentiating the expression in (
2.15
) with respect to
\bm{\phi}
, we have

T^{-1}(\bm{\Xi}\mathbf{Y}_{W}-\mathbf{B}^{\top}\mathbf{V})^{\top}(\bm{\Xi}\mathbf{Y}_{W}-\mathbf{B}^{\top}\mathbf{V})\bm{\phi}+T^{-1}(\bm{\Xi}\mathbf{Y}_{W}-\mathbf{B}^{\top}\mathbf{V})^{\top}(\mathbf{B}^{\top}\mathbf{y}-\bm{\Xi}\mathbf{y}^{\nu})=-\lambda\mathbf{h}.

Substituting (
2.11
) in the above, we arrive at

\displaystyle-\lambda\mathbf{h}

\displaystyle=T^{-1}(\bm{\Xi}\mathbf{Y}_{W}-\mathbf{B}^{\top}\mathbf{V})^{\top}(\bm{\Xi}\mathbf{Y}_{W}-\mathbf{B}^{\top}\mathbf{V})\bm{\phi}

\displaystyle\hskip 11.0pt+T^{-1}(\bm{\Xi}\mathbf{Y}_{W}-\mathbf{B}^{\top}\mathbf{V})^{\top}(\mathbf{B}^{\top}\mathbf{V}\bm{\phi}^{\ast}+\mathbf{B}^{\top}\mathbf{X}_{\bm{\beta}^{\ast}}\vec{\mathbf{I}_{d}}+\mathbf{B}^{\top}\bm{\epsilon}-\bm{\Xi}\mathbf{y}^{\nu})

\displaystyle=T^{-1}(\bm{\Xi}\mathbf{Y}_{W}-\mathbf{B}^{\top}\mathbf{V})^{\top}\mathbf{B}^{\top}\mathbf{V}(\bm{\phi}^{\ast}-\bm{\phi})+T^{-1}(\bm{\Xi}\mathbf{Y}_{W}-\mathbf{B}^{\top}\mathbf{V})^{\top}\mathbf{B}^{\top}\bm{\epsilon}

\displaystyle\hskip 12.0pt+T^{-1}(\bm{\Xi}\mathbf{Y}_{W}-\mathbf{B}^{\top}\mathbf{V})^{\top}\mathbf{B}^{\top}\mathbf{X}_{\bm{\beta}^{\ast}}\vec{\mathbf{I}_{d}}

\displaystyle\hskip 12.0pt+T^{-1}(\bm{\Xi}\mathbf{Y}_{W}-\mathbf{B}^{\top}\mathbf{V})^{\top}(\bm{\Xi}\mathbf{Y}_{W}\bm{\phi}^{\ast}-\bm{\Xi}\mathbf{y}^{\nu}+\bm{\Xi}\mathbf{Y}_{W}(\bm{\phi}-\bm{\phi}^{\ast}))

\displaystyle=T^{-1}(\bm{\Xi}\mathbf{Y}_{W}-\mathbf{B}^{\top}\mathbf{V})^{\top}(\bm{\Xi}\mathbf{Y}_{W}-\mathbf{B}^{\top}\mathbf{V})(\bm{\phi}-\bm{\phi}^{\ast})+T^{-1}(\bm{\Xi}\mathbf{Y}_{W}-\mathbf{B}^{\top}\mathbf{V})^{\top}\mathbf{B}^{\top}\bm{\epsilon}

\displaystyle\hskip 12.0pt+T^{-1}(\bm{\Xi}\mathbf{Y}_{W}-\mathbf{B}^{\top}\mathbf{V})^{\top}\mathbf{B}^{\top}\mathbf{X}_{\bm{\beta}^{\ast}-\bm{\beta}(\bm{\phi}^{\ast})}\vec{\mathbf{I}_{d}},

where the last equality used the fact that
\mathbf{B}^{\top}\mathbf{X}_{\bm{\beta}(\bm{\phi}^{\ast})}\vec{\mathbf{I}_{d}}=\bm{\Xi}\mathbf{y}^{\nu}-\bm{\Xi}\mathbf{Y}_{W}\bm{\phi}^{\ast}
from (
S3
). Then we may conclude that there exists a sign-consistent solution
\widehat{\bm{\phi}}
if and only if

\left\{\begin{array}[]{ll}-\lambda\mathbf{h}_{H}=T^{-1}(\bm{\Xi}\mathbf{Y}_{W,H}-\mathbf{B}^{\top}\mathbf{V}_{H})^{\top}(\bm{\Xi}\mathbf{Y}_{W,H}-\mathbf{B}^{\top}\mathbf{V}_{H})(\widehat{\bm{\phi}}-\bm{\phi}^{\ast})\\
\hskip 40.0pt+T^{-1}(\bm{\Xi}\mathbf{Y}_{W,H}-\mathbf{B}^{\top}\mathbf{V}_{H})^{\top}\mathbf{B}^{\top}\mathbf{X}_{\bm{\beta}^{\ast}-\bm{\beta}(\bm{\phi}^{\ast})}\vec{\mathbf{I}_{d}}+T^{-1}(\bm{\Xi}\mathbf{Y}_{W,H}-\mathbf{B}^{\top}\mathbf{V}_{H})^{\top}\mathbf{B}^{\top}\bm{\epsilon},\\
\lambda\mathbf{u}_{H^{c}}\geq\Big|T^{-1}(\bm{\Xi}\mathbf{Y}_{W,H^{c}}-\mathbf{B}^{\top}\mathbf{V}_{H^{c}})^{\top}\mathbf{B}^{\top}\mathbf{X}_{\bm{\beta}^{\ast}-\bm{\beta}(\bm{\phi}^{\ast})}\vec{\mathbf{I}_{d}}+T^{-1}(\bm{\Xi}\mathbf{Y}_{W,H^{c}}-\mathbf{B}^{\top}\mathbf{V}_{H^{c}})^{\top}\mathbf{B}^{\top}\bm{\epsilon}\Big|,\end{array}\right.

(S36)

where
\mathbf{A}_{H}
and
\mathbf{a}_{H}
denote the corresponding submatrix
\mathbf{A}
with columns restricted on the set
H
and subvector
\mathbf{a}
with entries restricted on the set
H
, respectively. Similarly
(\cdot)_{H^{c}}
is defined. Consider the first equation in (
S36
), similar to how
D_{2}
is decomposed in the proof of Theorem
1
, we write
\widehat{\bm{\phi}}-\bm{\phi}^{\ast}=\sum_{j=1}^{4}I_{\phi,j}
where

\displaystyle I_{\phi,1}

\displaystyle=[(\mathbf{H}_{20}-\mathbf{H}_{10})_{H}^{\top}(\mathbf{H}_{20}-\mathbf{H}_{10})_{H}]^{-1}\Big\{(\mathbf{H}_{20}-\mathbf{H}_{10})_{H}^{\top}(\mathbf{H}_{20}-\mathbf{H}_{10})_{H}

\displaystyle\hskip 12.0pt-T^{-1}d^{a}(\mathbf{B}^{\top}\mathbf{V}_{H}-\bm{\Xi}\mathbf{Y}_{W,H})^{\top}(\mathbf{B}^{\top}\mathbf{V}_{H}-\bm{\Xi}\mathbf{Y}_{W,H})\Big\}(\widehat{\bm{\phi}}-\bm{\phi}^{\ast}),

\displaystyle I_{\phi,2}

\displaystyle=-[(\mathbf{H}_{20}-\mathbf{H}_{10})_{H}^{\top}(\mathbf{H}_{20}-\mathbf{H}_{10})_{H}]^{-1}d^{a}\lambda\mathbf{h}_{H},

\displaystyle I_{\phi,3}

\displaystyle=[(\mathbf{H}_{20}-\mathbf{H}_{10})_{H}^{\top}(\mathbf{H}_{20}-\mathbf{H}_{10})_{H}]^{-1}T^{-1}d^{a}(\bm{\Xi}\mathbf{Y}_{W,H}-\mathbf{B}^{\top}\mathbf{V}_{H})^{\top}\mathbf{B}^{\top}\mathbf{X}_{\bm{\beta}(\bm{\phi}^{\ast})-\bm{\beta}^{\ast}}\vec{\mathbf{I}_{d}},

\displaystyle I_{\phi,4}

\displaystyle=[(\mathbf{H}_{20}-\mathbf{H}_{10})_{H}^{\top}(\mathbf{H}_{20}-\mathbf{H}_{10})_{H}]^{-1}T^{-1}d^{a}(\mathbf{B}^{\top}\mathbf{V}_{H}-\bm{\Xi}\mathbf{Y}_{W,H})^{\top}\mathbf{B}^{\top}\bm{\epsilon}.

Similar to
F_{1}
in the proof of Theorem
1
, we may derive that

\big\|I_{\phi,1}\big\|_{\text{max}}=O_{P}\Big(c_{T}d^{1-a}\big\|\widehat{\bm{\phi}}-\bm{\phi}^{\ast}\big\|_{\text{max}}\Big)=o_{P}\Big(\big\|\widehat{\bm{\phi}}-\bm{\phi}^{\ast}\big\|_{\text{max}}\Big),

(S37)

where the first equality used the fact that (R1) implies for a positive constant
u
that
\sigma_{|H|}^{2}\{(\mathbf{D}_{W})_{H}\}\geq du>0
uniformly as
d\to\infty
, and the conditions in the statement of Theorem
2
, and the second used (R10). Similarly, with Assumption (R9) we have

\big\|I_{\phi,2}\big\|_{\text{max}}=O_{P}\big(d^{-1-a}\cdot d^{a}\cdot\lambda\big)=O_{P}\big(c_{T}d^{-1}\big).

(S38)

For
I_{\phi,4}
, we may decompose it as the following with the second term dominating the first term similarly to
F_{5}
and
F_{6}
in the proof of Theorem
1
,

\displaystyle I_{\phi,4}

\displaystyle=\Big([(\mathbf{H}_{20}-\mathbf{H}_{10})_{H}^{\top}(\mathbf{H}_{20}-\mathbf{H}_{10})_{H}]^{-1}

\displaystyle\hskip 12.0pt\cdot\Big[(T^{-1/2}d^{a/2}\mathbf{B}^{\top}\mathbf{V}_{H}-\mathbf{H}_{10,H})-(T^{-1/2}d^{a/2}\bm{\Xi}\mathbf{Y}_{W,H}-\mathbf{H}_{20,H})\Big]^{\top}\cdot T^{-1/2}d^{a/2}\mathbf{B}^{\top}\bm{\epsilon}\Big)

\displaystyle\hskip 12.0pt-\Big([(\mathbf{H}_{20}-\mathbf{H}_{10})_{H}^{\top}(\mathbf{H}_{20}-\mathbf{H}_{10})_{H}]^{-1}(\mathbf{H}_{20}-\mathbf{H}_{10})_{H}^{\top}\cdot T^{-1/2}d^{a/2}\mathbf{B}^{\top}\bm{\epsilon}\Big).

The second term in the above has rate
T^{-1/2}d^{-(1+a-b)/2}
by exactly the same way to construct asymptotic normality of
F_{6}
in (
S35
), except for the restriction to the set
H
here (proof omitted). Thus,

\big\|I_{\phi,4}\big\|_{\text{max}}=O_{P}\big(T^{-1/2}d^{-(1+a-b)/2}\big).

(S39)

We next construct the asymptotic normality of
I_{\phi,3}
and show its rate is of order
T^{-1/2}d^{-(1-b)/2}
which is dominating over those of
I_{\phi,1}
,
I_{\phi,2}
and
I_{\phi,4}
by Assumption (R10). Recall
\mathbf{R}_{H}=[(\mathbf{H}_{20}-\mathbf{H}_{10})_{H}^{\top}(\mathbf{H}_{20}-\mathbf{H}_{10})_{H}]^{-1}(\mathbf{H}_{20}-\mathbf{H}_{10})_{H}^{\top}
, and let non-zero
\bm{\alpha}\in\mathbbm{R}^{|H|}
such that
\|\bm{\alpha}\|_{1}\leq c<\infty
. Then we have

\displaystyle\hskip 12.0pt\bm{\alpha}^{\top}I_{\phi,3}=\bm{\alpha}^{\top}\mathbf{R}_{H}T^{-1}(\mathbf{B}_{\bm{\gamma}}-\bar{\mathbf{B}}_{\bm{\gamma}})^{\top}\mathbf{X}_{\bm{\beta}(\bm{\phi}^{\ast})-\bm{\beta}^{\ast}}\text{vec}\big(\mathbf{I}_{d}\big)(1+o_{P}(1))

\displaystyle=\bm{\alpha}^{\top}\mathbf{R}_{H}T^{-1}\Big(\mathbf{I}_{d}\otimes\big\{(\mathbf{B}_{1}-\bar{\mathbf{B}},\dots,\mathbf{B}_{T}-\bar{\mathbf{B}})

\displaystyle\hskip 12.0pt\cdot(\mathbf{I}_{T}\otimes\bm{\gamma}(\bm{\beta}(\bm{\phi}^{\ast})-\bm{\beta}^{\ast})^{\top})(\mathbf{X}_{1},\dots,\mathbf{X}_{T})^{\top}\big\}\Big)\text{vec}\big(\mathbf{I}_{d}\big)(1+o_{P}(1))

\displaystyle=\bm{\alpha}^{\top}\mathbf{R}_{H}\,\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\text{vec}\big((\mathbf{B}_{t}-\mathbbm{E}(\mathbf{B}_{t}))\,\bm{\gamma}\,(\bm{\beta}(\bm{\phi}^{\ast})-\bm{\beta}^{\ast})^{\top}\mathbf{X}_{t}^{\top}\big)(1+o_{P}(1))

\displaystyle=\bm{\alpha}^{\top}\mathbf{R}_{H}\,\text{vec}\Big(\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\Big[\bm{\gamma}^{\top}(\mathbf{B}_{t,i\cdot}-\mathbbm{E}(\mathbf{B}_{t,i\cdot}))\mathbf{X}_{t,j\cdot}^{\top}(\bm{\beta}(\bm{\phi}^{\ast})-\bm{\beta}^{\ast})\Big]_{i,j\in[d]}\Big)(1+o_{P}(1))

\displaystyle=\bm{\alpha}^{\top}\mathbf{R}_{H}\mathbf{S}_{\bm{\gamma}}(\bm{\beta}(\bm{\phi}^{\ast})-\bm{\beta}^{\ast})(1+o_{P}(1)),

where the third last equality used
\mathcal{A}_{4}
in Lemma
2
and the last used
\mathcal{A}_{1}
. From (
S6
) and Lemma
4
, we have

\displaystyle\hskip 12.0ptT^{1/2}(\mathbf{R}_{\beta}\bm{\Sigma}_{\beta}\mathbf{R}_{\beta}^{\top})^{-1/2}(\bm{\beta}(\bm{\phi}^{\ast})-\bm{\beta}^{\ast})

\displaystyle=T^{\frac{1}{2}}(\mathbf{R}_{\beta}\bm{\Sigma}_{\beta}\mathbf{R}_{\beta}^{\top})^{-\frac{1}{2}}\Big\{\big(\mathbf{X}^{\top}\mathbf{B}^{\nu}(\mathbf{B}^{\nu})^{\top}\mathbf{X}\big)^{-1}\mathbf{X}^{\top}\mathbf{B}^{\nu}(\mathbf{B}^{\nu})^{\top}\bm{\epsilon}^{\nu}\Big\}

\displaystyle=T^{\frac{1}{2}}(\mathbf{R}_{\beta}\bm{\Sigma}_{\beta}\mathbf{R}_{\beta}^{\top})^{-\frac{1}{2}}\Big\{\big[\mathbbm{E}(\mathbf{X}_{t}^{\top}\mathbf{B}_{t})\mathbbm{E}(\mathbf{B}_{t}^{\top}\mathbf{X}_{t})\big]^{\text{-}1}\frac{1}{T^{2}}\mathbf{X}^{\top}\mathbf{B}^{\nu}(\mathbf{B}^{\nu})^{\top}\bm{\epsilon}^{\nu}(1+o(1))\Big\}\xrightarrow{D}\mathcal{N}(\mathbf{0},\mathbf{I}_{r}).

Define
s_{3}:=\bm{\alpha}^{\top}\mathbf{R}_{H}\mathbf{S}_{\bm{\gamma}}\mathbf{R}_{\beta}\bm{\Sigma}_{\beta}\mathbf{R}_{\beta}^{\top}\mathbf{S}_{\bm{\gamma}}^{\top}\mathbf{R}_{H}^{\top}\bm{\alpha}
, we hence have
T^{1/2}s_{3}^{-1/2}\bm{\alpha}^{\top}I_{\phi,3}\xrightarrow{D}\mathcal{N}(0,1)
and equivalently

T^{1/2}(\mathbf{R}_{H}\mathbf{S}_{\bm{\gamma}}\mathbf{R}_{\beta}\bm{\Sigma}_{\beta}\mathbf{R}_{\beta}^{\top}\mathbf{S}_{\bm{\gamma}}^{\top}\mathbf{R}_{H}^{\top})^{-1/2}I_{\phi,3}\xrightarrow{D}\mathcal{N}(\mathbf{0},\mathbf{I}_{|H|}).

As shown in the proof of Lemma
4
, the eigenvalues of
\mathbf{R}_{\beta}\bm{\Sigma}_{\beta}\mathbf{R}_{\beta}^{\top}
are of order
d^{b-1}
. Similar to
\mathbf{R}_{1}
in the proof of Theorem
1
,
\lambda_{\text{max}}(\mathbf{R}_{H}\mathbf{R}_{H}^{\top})=O(d^{-1-a})
. We also have
\lambda_{\text{max}}(\mathbf{S}_{\bm{\gamma}}^{\top}\mathbf{S}_{\bm{\gamma}})=O(d^{1+a})
by Assumption (R5). Combining them, we have

\displaystyle\hskip 12.0pt\|\bm{\alpha}\|_{1}^{2}\lambda_{\text{min}}(\mathbf{R}_{H}\mathbf{S}_{\bm{\gamma}}\mathbf{S}_{\bm{\gamma}}^{\top}\mathbf{R}_{H}^{\top})\lambda_{\text{min}}(\mathbf{R}_{\beta}\bm{\Sigma}_{\beta}\mathbf{R}_{\beta}^{\top})

\displaystyle\leq s_{3}\leq\|\bm{\alpha}\|_{1}^{2}\lambda_{\text{max}}(\mathbf{R}_{H}\mathbf{R}_{H}^{\top})\lambda_{\text{max}}(\mathbf{S}_{\bm{\gamma}}^{\top}\mathbf{S}_{\bm{\gamma}})\lambda_{\text{max}}(\mathbf{R}_{\beta}\bm{\Sigma}_{\beta}\mathbf{R}_{\beta}^{\top}),

with the right hand side of order
d^{b-1}
. The left hand side is of the same order by the assumption in the statement of Theorem
2
that
\mathbf{R}_{H}\mathbf{S}_{\bm{\gamma}}\mathbf{S}_{\bm{\gamma}}^{\top}\mathbf{R}_{H}^{\top}
has the smallest eigenvalue of constant order. Thus,
s_{3}
is of order exactly
d^{b-1}
and hence
\bm{\alpha}^{\top}I_{\phi,3}
has order exactly
T^{-1/2}d^{-(1-b)/2}
. It implies
I_{\phi,3}
is the leading term in
\widehat{\bm{\phi}}-\bm{\phi}^{\ast}
whose asymptotic normality therefore holds.

As
I_{\phi,1}
to
I_{\phi,4}
are all
o_{P}(1)
, we conclude
\text{sign}(\widehat{\bm{\phi}}_{H})=\text{sign}(\bm{\phi}_{H}^{\ast})
. It remains to show the second part in (
S36
) for the zero consistency of
\widehat{\bm{\phi}}_{H^{c}}
.

To this end, notice similar to
I_{\phi,3}
but with restriction on the set
H^{c}
, we have

\displaystyle\hskip 12.0pt\big\|T^{-1}(\bm{\Xi}\mathbf{Y}_{W,H^{c}}-\mathbf{B}^{\top}\mathbf{V}_{H^{c}})^{\top}\mathbf{B}^{\top}\mathbf{X}_{\bm{\beta}^{\ast}-\bm{\beta}(\bm{\phi}^{\ast})}\vec{\mathbf{I}_{d}}\big\|_{\text{max}}

\displaystyle=O_{P}\big(T^{-1/2}d^{-(1-b)/2}\cdot d^{-a}\cdot d^{1+2a}\big)=O_{P}\big(T^{-1/2}d^{\frac{1}{2}+\frac{b}{2}+a}\big),

which used
\big\|(\mathbf{H}_{20}-\mathbf{H}_{10})^{\top}(\mathbf{H}_{20}-\mathbf{H}_{10})\big\|_{\text{max}}\leq\sigma_{1}^{2}(\mathbf{H}_{20}-\mathbf{H}_{10})=O(d^{1+2a})
similarly to the steps above (
S15
). In the same manner, we also have from
I_{\phi,4}
that

\big\|T^{-1}(\bm{\Xi}\mathbf{Y}_{W,H^{c}}-\mathbf{B}^{\top}\mathbf{V}_{H^{c}})^{\top}\mathbf{B}^{\top}\bm{\epsilon}\big\|_{\text{max}}=O_{P}\big(T^{-1/2}d^{\frac{1}{2}+\frac{b}{2}+\frac{a}{2}}\big).

The left hand side of the second inequality in (
S36
) has minimum value of

\frac{\lambda}{\big\|\widetilde{\bm{\phi}}_{H^{c}}\big\|_{\text{max}}}\geq\frac{\lambda}{\big\|\widetilde{\bm{\phi}}_{H^{c}}-\bm{\phi}_{H^{c}}^{\ast}\big\|_{\text{max}}}\geq\frac{\lambda}{\big\|\widetilde{\bm{\phi}}-\bm{\phi}^{\ast}\big\|_{\text{max}}},

so it suffices to show

\big(T^{-1/2}d^{\frac{1}{2}+\frac{b}{2}+a}\big)\cdot\big\|\widetilde{\bm{\phi}}-\bm{\phi}^{\ast}\big\|_{\text{max}}=o_{P}(c_{T}),

which is true by Assumption (R10) and Theorem
1
in which each entry of
F_{6}
can be shown to be asymptotically normal. This completes the proof of Theorem
2
.

\square

Proof of Theorem
3
.

We have

\begin{split}\big\|\widehat{\mathbf{W}}_{t}-\mathbf{W}_{t}^{\ast}\big\|_{\infty}&=\bigg\|\sum_{j=1}^{p}\Big[(\widehat{\phi}_{j,0}-\phi_{j,0}^{\ast})+\sum_{k=1}^{l_{j}}(\widehat{\phi}_{j,k}-\phi_{j,k}^{\ast})z_{j,k,t}\Big]\mathbf{W}_{j}\bigg\|_{\infty}\\
&=O_{P}\Big(\big\|\widehat{\bm{\phi}}-\bm{\phi}^{\ast}\big\|_{1}\cdot\max_{j}\|\mathbf{W}_{j}\|_{\infty}\Big)=O_{P}\big(T^{-1/2}d^{-(1-b)/2}\big),\end{split}

(S40)

where the last equality used Theorem
2
, Assumptions (M2) (or (M2’)) and (R1). Observe that we have similarly
\big\|\widehat{\mathbf{W}}_{t}-\mathbf{W}_{t}^{\ast}\big\|_{1}=O_{P}\big(T^{-1/2}d^{-(1-b)/2}\big)
by Assumption (R1), and hence

\big\|\widehat{\mathbf{W}}_{t}-\mathbf{W}_{t}^{\ast}\big\|\leq\Big(\big\|\widehat{\mathbf{W}}_{t}-\mathbf{W}_{t}^{\ast}\big\|_{1}\,\big\|\widehat{\mathbf{W}}_{t}-\mathbf{W}_{t}^{\ast}\big\|_{\infty}\Big)^{1/2}=O_{P}\big(T^{-1/2}d^{-(1-b)/2}\big).

With
\bm{\Pi}_{t}^{\ast}
defined in (
S8
), we can decompose

\displaystyle\widehat{\bm{\mu}}-\bm{\mu}^{\ast}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\Big\{(\mathbf{I}_{d}-\bm{\Lambda}_{t}\widehat{\bm{\Phi}})\mathbf{y}_{t}-\mathbf{X}_{t}\widehat{\bm{\beta}}\Big\}-\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\Big\{(\mathbf{I}_{d}-\bm{\Lambda}_{t}\bm{\Phi}^{\ast})\mathbf{y}_{t}-\mathbf{X}_{t}\bm{\beta}^{\ast}-\bm{\epsilon}_{t}\Big\}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\Big\{\big(\mathbf{W}_{t}^{\ast}-\widehat{\mathbf{W}}_{t}\big)\big(\bm{\Pi}_{t}^{\ast}\bm{\mu}^{\ast}+\bm{\Pi}_{t}^{\ast}\mathbf{X}_{t}\bm{\beta}^{\ast}+\bm{\Pi}_{t}^{\ast}\bm{\epsilon}_{t}\big)\Big\}+\bar{\mathbf{X}}(\bm{\beta}^{\ast}-\widehat{\bm{\beta}})+\bar{\bm{\epsilon}},

so that combining (
S40
), Lemma
2
and Theorem
1
, we have

\displaystyle\big\|\widehat{\bm{\mu}}-\bm{\mu}^{\ast}\big\|_{\text{max}}

\displaystyle=O_{P}\Big\{\max_{t}\big\|\widehat{\mathbf{W}}_{t}-\mathbf{W}_{t}^{\ast}\big\|_{\infty}\Big(\big\|\bm{\mu}^{\ast}\big\|_{\text{max}}+c_{T}\big\|\bm{\beta}^{\ast}\big\|_{\text{max}}+c_{T}\Big)

\displaystyle\hskip 125.0pt+c_{T}\big\|\bm{\beta}^{\ast}-\widehat{\bm{\beta}}\big\|_{1}+c_{T}\Big\}=O_{P}(c_{T}).

This completes the proof of Theorem
3
.
\square
